<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://motorolanews.com/motorola-three-new-b2b-solutions-at-mwc-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Motorola announces a partnership with GrapheneOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>摩托罗拉宣布与 GrapheneOS 基金会达成合作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47214645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47214645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 860</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Motorola teams up with the GrapheneOS Foundation to focus on security-hardened Android foundations and open-source collaboration. The move could influence future device security updates, transparency, and baseline protections across Motorola hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>摩托罗拉与 GrapheneOS 基金会达成合作，表明在 Android 安全加固与开源协作方面的重点。此举可能影响未来的安全更新、透明度以及硬件基础安全特性的建立。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://motorolanews.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/2024_MOTOROLA_B2B_PHOTOS_150dpi_44-1.png' data-full='https://motorolanews.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/2024_MOTOROLA_B2B_PHOTOS_150dpi_44-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47247645' data-tags='Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/say-hello-to-macbook-neo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MacBook Neo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果推出 MacBook Neo</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47247645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1940</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47247645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple officially launches MacBook Neo, a budget-friendly model powered by A18 Pro, targeting students and light workloads. The device promises improved energy efficiency and a more accessible gateway into Apple&#x27;s ecosystem, potentially expanding its mass-market share.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果正式推出 MacBook Neo，搭载 A18 Pro 芯片，定位入门级，面向学生与轻量级工作。新机承诺提升能效并降低入门门槛，或对苹果在大众市场的份额产生积极影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/say-hello-to-macbook-neo/tile/Apple-MacBook-Neo-hero-260304-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603041429' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/say-hello-to-macbook-neo/tile/Apple-MacBook-Neo-hero-260304-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603041429' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47202708' data-tags='AI,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://karpathy.github.io/2026/02/12/microgpt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microgpt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MicroGPT：轻量级 GPT 的新思路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1847</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 309</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact framework for running GPT-like agents on modest hardware. Emphasizes modular tooling, lightweight memory, and efficient orchestration to enable on-device AI and edge applications. This approach could lower barriers to deploying autonomous AI in real-world products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在中等算力设备上运行 GPT 风格代理的紧凑框架。强调模块化工具、轻量级内存和高效编排，以实现边缘设备上的 AI 自主执行。该思路可能降低在真实产品中部署自治 AI 的门槛。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.svd.se/a/K8nrV4/metas-ai-smart-glasses-and-data-privacy-concerns-workers-say-we-see-everything' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta’s AI smart glasses and data privacy concerns</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 的 AI 智能眼镜与数据隐私担忧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47225130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47225130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 788</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s AI glasses spark privacy debates as workers report pervasive sensing and potential data collection. The piece weighs the balance between convenience and surveillance, and what it means for corporate wearables and future AR devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 的 AI 智能眼镜引发隐私争议，员工反映设备在日常使用中存在广泛感知和潜在数据收集。文章讨论便利性与监控之间的权衡，以及这类企业级穿戴设备和未来 AR 设备的治理含义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://svd.vgc.no/v2/images/e4d3cfec-f7dc-4461-aa32-108ea47f2621?h=630&amp;q=80&amp;upscale=true&amp;w=1200&amp;s=d82dc447eb031914967ea232d0aab8207eab5ef0' data-full='https://svd.vgc.no/v2/images/e4d3cfec-f7dc-4461-aa32-108ea47f2621?h=630&amp;q=80&amp;upscale=true&amp;w=1200&amp;s=d82dc447eb031914967ea232d0aab8207eab5ef0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47241551' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/116160393783585567' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Motorola GrapheneOS devices will be bootloader unlockable/relockable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>摩托罗拉的 GrapheneOS 设备将支持解锁与重新锁定引导加载程序</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47241551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1244</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47241551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 527</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Motorola&#x27;s GrapheneOS devices will support bootloader unlock and relock, restoring user control over the device&#x27;s software stack while keeping the security workflow intact. This enables custom ROM flashing and safer recovery paths, aligned with GrapheneOS&#x27;s privacy and hardening goals. It also tests how manufacturers balance openness with enterprise security expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>摩托罗拉的 GrapheneOS 设备将支持解锁并重新锁定引导加载程序，重新赋予用户对软件栈的控制，同时保持安全更新流程完备。此举让刷入自定义 ROM 和更安全的设备恢复成为可能，与 GrapheneOS 的隐私与强化安全目标相契合。也反映出厂商在开放性与企业安全之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47275088' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.researchsquare.com/article/rs-6079807/v1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Global warming has accelerated significantly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球变暖显著加速</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New measurements show the pace of global warming has accelerated beyond prior projections, delivering record-high temperatures and more frequent extreme weather events. The finding underscores the urgency for rapid decarbonization and climate-tech innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新数据显示全球变暖速率显著加快，突破以往预测，伴随创纪录高温和更频繁的极端天气。该发现凸显了迅速降低碳排放和推动气候科技创新的紧迫性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.researchsquare.com/static/img/logos/logo-rs.svg' data-full='https://www.researchsquare.com/static/img/logos/logo-rs.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47223620' data-tags='Politics,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbc.ca/news/canada/british-columbia/b-c-adopting-year-round-daylight-time-9.7111657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>British Columbia is permanently adopting daylight time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不列颠哥伦比亚省永久采用夏令时</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47223620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47223620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 553</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>British Columbia will permanently observe daylight time, ending seasonal clock changes. The move aims to simplify scheduling and energy planning, but could complicate activities tied to daylight patterns and cross-border coordination with the US and other provinces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>不列颠哥伦比亚省将永久采用夏令时，取消季节性时钟调整。此举旨在简化日程与能源规划，但可能让依赖日照模式的活动和跨境协调变得更复杂。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47216047' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.windowslatest.com/2026/03/02/microsoft-gets-tired-of-microslop-bans-the-word-on-its-discord-then-locks-the-server-after-backlash/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Microslop” filtered in the official Microsoft Copilot Discord server</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>官方 Copilot Discord 服务器屏蔽“Microslop”一词</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47216047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47216047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 514</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft blocks the term &#x27;Microslop&#x27; in its Copilot Discord server, then locks the channel after backlash. The incident underscores tensions between branding, community moderation, and how corporate AI products are managed in public spaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软在官方 Copilot Discord 服务器屏蔽了“Microslop”一词，遭遇反对后又封锁服务器。此事件凸显品牌控制、社区治理以及在公开场合管理企业 AI 产品的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.windowslatest.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Copilots-official-Discord-channel-was-locked-down-after-users-went-haywire-with-unflattering-nicknames-targetted-at-Microsoft.jpg' data-full='https://www.windowslatest.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Copilots-official-Discord-channel-was-locked-down-after-users-went-haywire-with-unflattering-nicknames-targetted-at-Microsoft.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/politics/policy/judge-orders-government-to-begin-refunding-more-than-130-billion-in-tariffs-fdc1e62c' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Judge orders government to begin refunding more than $130B in tariffs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法官命令政府开始退还超过1300亿美元的关税</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47261688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1056</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47261688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 762</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A judge ordered the government to begin refunding more than $130 billion in tariffs collected over the years, a move that could ease costs for importers and reshape trade dynamics. The ruling may accelerate how swiftly refunds are processed and influence ongoing tariff policy debates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法官命令政府开始退还累计超过1300亿美元的关税。这笔退款将减轻进口商成本，并可能重塑关税政策的执行节奏与相关讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47263323' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wikimediastatus.net' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wikipedia was in read-only mode following mass admin account compromise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>维基百科因大规模管理员账户被入侵而进入只读模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1038</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 376</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mass admin account compromise forced Wikipedia into read-only mode to protect content integrity. The incident underscores the risk of credential breaches in high-privilege accounts and the need for rapid incident response, MFA enforcement, and auditing for large collaborative platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大量管理员账户被入侵，迫使维基百科进入只读模式以防止进一步编辑。此事件凸显高权限账户安全风险，以及快速处置、强制多因素认证和审计的重要性，尤其对大型协作型百科平台。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-transactional_logos/retina/233622/2560px-Wikimedia_Foundation_logo_-_horizontal.svg.png' data-full='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-transactional_logos/retina/233622/2560px-Wikimedia_Foundation_logo_-_horizontal.svg.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47282777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: I&#x27;m 60 years old. Claude Code has re-ignited a passion</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 60-year-old coder shares how Claude Code rekindled their love for programming, illustrating AI copilots&#x27; potential to re-engage seasoned developers. It spotlights the human dimension of AI-assisted work and the value of reusable, long‑term motivation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位60岁程序员分享，Claude Code 重燃了他对编程的热情，体现 AI 辅助工具在激励老练开发者方面的潜力。也提醒我们，AI 助手的价值在于激发人们对长期学习与创作的热情。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/JosephPolitano/status/2029916364664611242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tech employment now significantly worse than the 2008 or 2020 recessions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技行业就业现状显著低于2008年和2020年衰退期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1002</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 677</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tech labor market deteriorated more sharply than during the 2008 financial crisis or the 2020 recession, with hiring slowdowns and rising layoffs across the sector. The trend raises questions about demand for tech talent, AI adoption, and policy responses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科技行业的就业市场比2008年金融危机或2020年衰退期间更为疲弱，出现招聘放缓和裁员增加。这一趋势引发对技术需求、AI应用和政策应对的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HCu0mG9bEAAdzYM.png:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HCu0mG9bEAAdzYM.png:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI releases GPT-5.4 with iterative improvements focused on safety, efficiency, and alignment for enterprise use. The update signals ongoing maturation of the GPT family as companies increasingly rely on AI assistants for critical workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 发布 GPT-5.4，强调在安全性、效率和对齐方面的改进，面向企业应用场景。此更新体现了 GPT 系列的持续成熟，以及企业对 AI 助手在关键工作流中的依赖增加。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47232768' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neilzone.co.uk/2026/03/im-struggling-to-think-of-any-online-services-for-which-id-be-willing-to-verify-my-identity-or-age/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m reluctant to verify my identity or age for any online services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不愿意在任何在线服务上验证身份或年龄</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 964</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 613</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Verifying identity or age has become a friction point in many online services, and I’m reluctant to do it. The post argues that invasive KYC checks erode privacy and push users away, and it hints at privacy-preserving alternatives and the broader trend toward minimal verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在线服务对身份/年龄的验证日益增多，造成用户体验负担。该文指出强制性的身份核验侵蚀隐私、抑制使用意愿，并探讨更隐私友好的身份认证替代方案以及数字身份的未来趋势。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/googleworkspace/cli' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Workspace CLI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Workspace 命令行工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47255881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 916</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47255881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 285</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Google Workspace CLI project provides a command-line interface for automating and scripting Workspace admin tasks. It enables developers to manage users, drive configurations, and integrate with existing CI/CD workflows. The move aligns with a broader push to make cloud admin workflows more programmable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Workspace CLI 提供了用于自动化和脚本化管理任务的命令行界面，方便开发者对用户、配置等执行操作并集成到 CI/CD 流水线。此举体现云端管理工作流向可编程化的趋势，提升运维效率。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47252971' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mas.to/@gabrielesvelto/116171750653898304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>10% of Firefox crashes are caused by bitflips</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 崩溃中有 10% 由位翻转引发</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47252971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 875</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47252971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 458</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports that about 10% of Firefox crashes are caused by bitflips in memory, underscoring the impact of hardware faults on software reliability. The finding motivates better error handling, ECC memory use, and robust testing for resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道显示约 10% 的 Firefox 崩溃由内存位翻转引起，凸显硬件故障对软件可靠性的影响。该发现促使加强错误处理、使用 ECC 内存以及提高容错测试。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mas.to/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://mas.to/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47246110' data-tags='Career,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terriblesoftware.org/2026/03/03/nobody-gets-promoted-for-simplicity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobody gets promoted for simplicity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没人因为简单而升职</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47246110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 852</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47246110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 490</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion piece argues that promotions in many tech organizations reward complexity and visibility rather than keeping systems simple. The author points to entrenched incentives that favor adding features, optimization, or risk-taking over clean, minimal design. The takeaway is to prioritize clarity, reliability, and lean architectures to unlock long-term value and career advancement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇观点文章认为，许多科技组织的晋升机制偏向复杂性和可见度，而非坚持简单设计。作者指出习以成年的激励让人们更重视增添功能、优化和冒险，而不是追求清晰、稳定的系统。核心信息是，长远价值来自清晰、可靠、简化的架构，有助于职业发展的提升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/terriblesoftware.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/chjpdmf0zs9sci9pbwfnzxmvd2vic2l0zs8ymdiylta1l25zmtexndetaw1hz2uta3d2d3b1c3kuanbn.webp?fit=1024%2C680&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/terriblesoftware.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/chjpdmf0zs9sci9pbwfnzxmvd2vic2l0zs8ymdiylta1l25zmtexndetaw1hz2uta3d2d3b1c3kuanbn.webp?fit=1024%2C680&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/apple-introduces-macbook-pro-with-all-new-m5-pro-and-m5-max/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MacBook Pro with M5 Pro and M5 Max</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>搭载全新 M5 Pro 与 M5 Max 的 MacBook Pro</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 849</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 939</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple introduces the MacBook Pro with the new M5 Pro and M5 Max chips, signaling another generational leap in performance and efficiency. Pro workflows like 3D rendering, video editing, and ML workloads should see meaningful speedups, with improved energy efficiency and media acceleration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出搭载全新 M5 Pro/Max 的 MacBook Pro，预示性能与能效进入新一代。3D 渲染、视频编辑和机器学习等专业工作流有望显著提速，同时提升媒体处理能力与续航表现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/apple-introduces-macbook-pro-with-all-new-m5-pro-and-m5-max/tile/Apple-MacBook-Pro-M5-Pro-and-M5-Max-Capture-One-260303-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603031721' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/apple-introduces-macbook-pro-with-all-new-m5-pro-and-m5-max/tile/Apple-MacBook-Pro-M5-Pro-and-M5-Max-Capture-One-260303-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603031721' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ghostty.org/docs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ghostty – Terminal Emulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ghostty 终端模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 846</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 355</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ghostty is a modern terminal emulator project aimed at boosting UX with features like multi-tab support and performance improvements. For power users, a refined terminal experience can significantly boost productivity across desktop and mobile workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ghostty 是一个面向提升用户体验的现代终端模拟器项目，可能包含多标签等功能和性能改进。对资深开发者而言，优化的终端体验能显著提升桌面与移动工作流程的生产力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47270784' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.system76.com/post/system76-on-age-verification/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>System76 on Age Verification Laws</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>System76 对年龄验证法的看法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47270784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 825</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47270784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 581</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>System76 argues that age-verification requirements risk privacy and accessibility for users and developers, potentially burdening open-source hardware and software ecosystems. The post calls for balanced policy that protects vulnerable users without stifling innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>System76 指出年龄验证要求可能侵犯隐私、降低可访问性，并给开源硬件和软件生态带来额外负担。文中呼吁在保护脆弱用户与不扼杀创新之间实现政策平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.system76.com/images/social.jpg' data-full='https://blog.system76.com/images/social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47230710' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu/~knuth/papers/claude-cycles.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude&#x27;s Cycles [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 的循环（PDF）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 785</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 333</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stanford/Knuth-hosted paper examines cycles in Claude, offering formal analysis and possibly algorithmic insights. It presents a theoretical exploration of repetitive structures in Claude’s context, with formal proofs or demonstrations. The work contributes to understanding computational cycles and their implications in Claude’s framework.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份由斯坦福/ Knuth 团队整理的论文探讨了 Claude 中的循环现象，给出形式化分析与潜在的算法洞见。内容聚焦 Claude 语境下的重复结构，包含证明或演示。该研究有助于理解计算循环及其在 Claude 框架中的意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grith.ai/blog/clinejection-when-your-ai-tool-installs-another' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A GitHub Issue Title Compromised 4k Developer Machines</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个 GitHub 问题标题被劫持，影响约4千台开发者机器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 615</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A blog suggests that a crafted GitHub issue title could trigger an injection in an AI-assisted workflow, potentially compromising thousands of developer machines. The post underscores the need for input sanitization, strict tool boundaries, and provenance verification when AI is integrated with code hosting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇博文指出，通过设计的 GitHub issue 标题可能在 AI 助手工作流中触发命令注入，进而影响数千名开发者的机器。此事强调在将 AI 与代码托管服务结合使用时需加强输入过滤、边界控制和来源可信度的验证。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47273854' data-tags='Security,AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/mozilla-firefox-security' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hardening Firefox with Anthropic&#x27;s Red Team</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>与 Anthropic 红队联合加强 Firefox 安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47273854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 599</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47273854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla recruited Anthropic&#x27;s red team to test Firefox against adversarial threats, uncovering vulnerabilities and proposing hardening strategies. The effort demonstrates how external red-team exercises can raise browser security in a era of AI-assisted attacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 邀请 Anthropic 红队对 Firefox 进行对抗性测试，揭示漏洞并提出加固策略。此举展示了在 AI 驱动威胁日益猖獗的背景下，外部红队演练如何提升浏览器安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.mozilla.org/wp-content/blogs.dir/278/files/2026/03/Mozilla_Illustrations_Pixelgram_Lock_Square_Orange-1080x720.png' data-full='https://blog.mozilla.org/wp-content/blogs.dir/278/files/2026/03/Mozilla_Illustrations_Pixelgram_Lock_Square_Orange-1080x720.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47274676' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.cornell.edu/stories/2026/03/workers-who-love-synergizing-paradigms-might-be-bad-their-jobs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Workers who love ‘synergizing paradigms’ might be bad at their jobs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>喜欢‘协同范式’的员工可能不适合本职工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47274676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 597</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47274676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 327</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Cornell piece suggests that teams heavy on buzzwords like synergizing paradigms may underperform, emphasizing concrete problem-solving over abstract rhetoric. The message resonates in tech culture where clear outcomes beat grand metaphors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cornell 的研究称，过分依赖“协同范式”等空洞术语的员工可能业绩不佳，强调解决问题的具体性比空谈更重要。这一观点在科技团队中尤为相关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.cornell.edu/sites/default/files/styles/story_thumbnail_lg/public/2026-03/0302_corporatebs_tnd.jpg?itok=HH3QjqWe' data-full='https://news.cornell.edu/sites/default/files/styles/story_thumbnail_lg/public/2026-03/0302_corporatebs_tnd.jpg?itok=HH3QjqWe' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47226608' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://futurism.com/artificial-intelligence/ars-technica-fires-reporter-ai-quotes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ars Technica fires reporter after AI controversy involving fabricated quotes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ars Technica 因 AI 造假引发的争议解雇记者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47226608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 586</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47226608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 367</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A journalist is fired after allegations that quotes attributed to AI were fabricated. The incident underscores the need for rigorous verification when using AI in journalism and the ethics of AI-assisted reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>因涉及 AI 捏造引述的争议，记者被解雇。这一事件凸显在新闻报道中使用 AI 时，需进行严格的核实与伦理考量，以及对AI生成内容的信任风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://futurism.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/ars-technica-fires-reporter-ai-quotes.jpg?w=1200' data-full='https://futurism.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/ars-technica-fires-reporter-ai-quotes.jpg?w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cjd98091g28o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US economy unexpectedly sheds 92k jobs in February</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>二月美国经济意外减少9.2万个工作岗位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 562</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 772</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>February payroll data surprised to the downside, shedding about 92,000 jobs as hiring cooled across sectors. The softer labor market could influence Fed policy expectations and AI-driven productivity gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>二月就业数据意外下降约9.2万个岗位，招聘在各领域放缓。这一趋势可能影响美联储的货币政策预期，并对 AI 驱动的生产力成长路径产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6efa/live/41eb38e0-1962-11f1-a1cd-a71e69f1cf89.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6efa/live/41eb38e0-1962-11f1-a1cd-a71e69f1cf89.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ogirardot.writizzy.com/p/good-software-knows-when-to-stop' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Good software knows when to stop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>好软件知道何时该停下来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47261561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 535</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47261561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A piece argues for restraint in software, warning against feature creep and unnecessary complexity. Focus on reliability, security, and maintainability—letting tools do a few things well rather than many mediocre ones.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张软件开发需要克制，反对无休止的功能堆叠与复杂性。强调以可靠性、安保和可维护性为核心，让工具专注做好几件事，而不是堆砌大量功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ogirardot.writizzy.com/__og-image__/image/p/good-software-knows-when-to-stop/og.png' data-full='https://ogirardot.writizzy.com/__og-image__/image/p/good-software-knows-when-to-stop/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/guides/agentic-engineering-patterns/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agentic Engineering Patterns</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A primer on building AI-assisted systems using agentic patterns—designs that orchestrate agents, prompts, and tools to perform complex tasks. The piece outlines practical patterns to balance autonomy, reliability, and controllability as AI agents collaborate with software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍用代理式模式构建 AI 辅助系统的方法，强调通过代理、提示与工具的协同来完成复杂任务。提出在自主性、可靠性与可控性之间取得平衡的实用设计模式。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/Gavriel_Cohen/status/2028821432759717930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m losing the SEO battle for my own open source project</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在自己的开源项目的 SEO 竞争中落败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 522</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 258</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A prominent open-source maintainer laments SEO challenges and discovers that organic search visibility can dwarf good code. The post outlines why docs, SEO hygiene, and credible references matter for discoverability and offers pragmatic steps to improve search presence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这位开源项目维护者对 SEO 竞争发出感叹，指出良好的代码并不必然带来可观的搜索能见度。文中强调文档、SEO 规范和可信引用在发现性中的重要性，并给出提升搜索存在感的实用建议。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47204964' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mlu-explain.github.io/decision-tree/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decision trees – the unreasonable power of nested decision rules</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>决策树：嵌套决策规则的强大力量</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 521</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nested decision rules let a decision tree encode nuanced logic with transparent paths, but depth can make them unwieldy. With careful structure and pruning, they remain a practical tool for governable, explainable rules even as data-driven models rise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>嵌套决策规则使决策树能够以透明的路径表达细致的逻辑，但深度增加会使其变得难以维护。通过合适的结构和剪枝，它仍然是可解释性强、可治理的规则工具，即便在数据驱动模型盛行时也有用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mlu-explain.github.io/assets/ogimages/ogimage-decision-tree.png' data-full='https://mlu-explain.github.io/assets/ogimages/ogimage-decision-tree.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=B3m3AMRlYfc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Physics Girl: Super-Kamiokande – Imaging the sun by detecting neutrinos [video]</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47233110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 508</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47233110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A physics explainer video on how the Super-Kamiokande detector images the Sun by detecting neutrinos, illustrating how particle physics probes solar processes. The video translates complex detector science into intuition about neutrino astronomy and why it matters for understanding fusion in the Sun.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该视频讲解通过探测中微子来成像太阳，展示大规模探测器如何揭示太阳的核聚变过程。内容将高能物理与天文学交汇，帮助理解中微子天文学的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/cbp-tapped-into-the-online-advertising-ecosystem-to-track-peoples-movements/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CBP tapped into the online advertising ecosystem to track peoples’ movements</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reveals CBP’s use of the online advertising ecosystem to track movements, raising privacy and civil liberties concerns. The finding spotlights how commercial data flows enable government surveillance and the need for policy guardrails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示海关执法机构利用在线广告生态系统追踪民众行动，引发隐私与公民自由担忧。强调商业数据流对政府监控的作用及需要政策约束。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/chardet/chardet/issues/327' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No right to relicense this project</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无权重新授权此项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47259177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 496</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47259177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 345</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses licensing rights around relicensing a project, arguing that the author cannot unilaterally relicense without consent or that relicensing rights are contested. The post underscores how licensing choices affect community adoption and collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨关于对一个项目进行再授权的许可权问题，认为作者在未获得同意的情况下不能单方面改变授权，或指出再授权权利存在争议。文章强调许可选择会影响社区采纳与协作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fdfd0c006133a5958b03d2471b00af07661ea95f2bdb2ba47b022fc9ecd2b36f/chardet/chardet/issues/327' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fdfd0c006133a5958b03d2471b00af07661ea95f2bdb2ba47b022fc9ecd2b36f/chardet/chardet/issues/327' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47240694' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ifixit.com/News/115827/new-thinkpads-score-perfect-10-repairability' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lenovo’s new ThinkPads score 10/10 for repairability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lenovo 新 ThinkPads 获得维修性满分 10/10</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47240694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 494</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47240694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 237</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>iFixit rates Lenovo ThinkPads with a perfect 10 for repairability, signaling strong serviceability in upgradeable components and modular design. The review highlights replaceable batteries, accessible screws, and easy part swaps as durable, repair-friendly design practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>iFixit 给出联想 ThinkPad 的维修性满分 10/10，体现其模块化设计与易于维护的部件替换性。评测强调可更换电池、易于打开的螺丝和便捷的部件更换等特性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47300329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Please restrict new accounts from posting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：请限制新账户发帖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47300329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 494</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47300329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 383</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ask HN asks for restricting new accounts from posting, touching on moderation, spam suppression, and the trade-offs of gating new users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ask HN 提出限制新账户发帖的设想，涉及版面治理、垃圾信息压制以及对新用户的门槛影响等权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47212355' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mandel-macaque/memento' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If AI writes code, should the session be part of the commit?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果 AI 写代码，会话应成为提交的一部分吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47212355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 479</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47212355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 382</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>If AI-assisted sessions influence code changes, teams may consider embedding session provenance in commits or metadata for traceability. This raises questions about reproducibility, review, and auditing of AI-influenced work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>若 AI 辅助的编辑会影响代码变更，团队可考虑在提交中嵌入会话出处或元数据以实现可追溯性。这引发关于可复现性、审查与对 AI 影响的审计等问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4a1a8271a17ff314e52296c69b038ab32c67345273a2a415e7b8b9af6906c84d/mandel-macaque/memento' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4a1a8271a17ff314e52296c69b038ab32c67345273a2a415e7b8b9af6906c84d/mandel-macaque/memento' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47264756' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://paulgraham.com/brandage.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Brand Age</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>品牌时代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47264756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 478</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47264756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 366</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paul Graham argues that the era of branding is as crucial as product skills, shaping how startups gain trust and scale. He contends that a strong brand can unlock network effects and resilience in competitive markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>保罗·格雷厄姆认为品牌时代与产品能力同等重要，塑造初创企业如何获得信任和扩张。他指出强品牌能够在竞争激烈的市场中释放网络效应与韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.turbifycdn.com/aah/paulgraham/bel-7.gif' data-full='https://s.turbifycdn.com/aah/paulgraham/bel-7.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wltx.com/article/news/nation-world/281-53d8693e-77a4-42ad-86e4-3426a30d25ae' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everett shuts down Flock camera network after judge rules footage public record</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Everett 关闭 Flock 摄像头网络，法院裁定录像可公开记录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47213764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 470</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47213764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court ruling that FLOCK camera footage must become public records prompts authorities to shut down the network, highlighting tensions between surveillance, privacy, and accountability. The move underscores legal risk for city surveillance programs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院裁定 FLOCK 监控录像应作为公开记录，促使相关部门关闭网络，凸显监控、隐私与问责之间的紧张关系。此举凸显城市级监控项目的法律风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wltx.com/assets/KING/images/a34148ae-a136-4198-91d1-811853233a86/20260228T004510/a34148ae-a136-4198-91d1-811853233a86_1140x641.jpg' data-full='https://media.wltx.com/assets/KING/images/a34148ae-a136-4198-91d1-811853233a86/20260228T004510/a34148ae-a136-4198-91d1-811853233a86_1140x641.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://commerce.jolla.com/products/jolla-phone-sept-26' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jolla phone – a full-stack European alternative</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jolla 手机——全栈式欧洲替代方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47216037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 465</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47216037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jolla&#x27;s phone with Sailfish OS positions a European alternative to dominant platforms, emphasizing privacy and open standards, though facing app ecosystem challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jolla 的手机以 Sailfish OS 为基础，作为对主流平台的欧洲替代，强调隐私与开放标准，但在应用生态方面仍有挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://commerce.jolla.com/cdn/shop/files/jolla_phone_hero_double.png?v=1772105235' data-full='http://commerce.jolla.com/cdn/shop/files/jolla_phone_hero_double.png?v=1772105235' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47285960' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://torrentfreak.com/uploading-pirated-books-via-bittorrent-qualifies-as-fair-use-meta/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Uploading Pirated Books via BitTorrent Qualifies as Fair Use, Meta Argues</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 主张通过 BitTorrent 上传盗版书可构成合理使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47285960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 435</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47285960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta argues that uploading pirated books via BitTorrent should qualify as fair use, a stance that could reshape how courts view user-generated sharing and platform liability. The argument underscores the tension between copyright enforcement and transformative use in the digital commons.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 主张通过 BitTorrent 上传盗版书籍应被认定为合理使用，这一立场如果被采纳，可能改变法院对用户分享与平台责任的判定。此举凸显数字环境中版权保护与转化性使用之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://torrentfreak.com/images/books-long.png' data-full='https://torrentfreak.com/images/books-long.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47283337' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.katanaquant.com/p/your-llm-doesnt-write-correct-code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs work best when the user defines their acceptance criteria first</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在使用大型语言模型时，先定义接受标准效果最佳</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47283337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 435</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47283337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Defining acceptance criteria before prompting an LLM dramatically improves reliability and repeatability, helping teams avoid hallucinations and scope creep. Practically, it encourages writing tests, constraints, and evaluation before generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在提示前先定义接受标准能显著提升大语言模型输出的可靠性，减少错觉与范围漂移。实践要点包括编写测试、设置约束和评估准则，再生成结果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!b3NO!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F503cef77-5d11-4c3b-8b94-ef3a09599d87_1212x811.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!b3NO!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F503cef77-5d11-4c3b-8b94-ef3a09599d87_1212x811.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/apple-introduces-the-new-ipad-air-powered-by-m4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New iPad Air, powered by M4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新款 iPad Air，搭载 M4 芯片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 434</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 674</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils a new iPad Air powered by the M4, signaling continued silicon cadence and improved performance-per-watt for mobile devices. The move reinforces Apple&#x27;s silicon cadence and cross-device AI strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果发布搭载 M4 芯片的新款 iPad Air，意味着移动设备性能与能效的提升，并映射出苹果在各产品线统一 AI 计算与效率的策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/apple-introduces-the-new-ipad-air-powered-by-m4/tile/Apple-iPad-Air-M4-multitasking-260302-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603021433' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/apple-introduces-the-new-ipad-air-powered-by-m4/tile/Apple-iPad-Air-M4-multitasking-260302-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603021433' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47208398' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ejholmes.github.io/2026/02/28/mcp-is-dead-long-live-the-cli.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When does MCP make sense vs CLI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>什么时候 MCP 比 CLI 更合适</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47208398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 431</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47208398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparison of when a higher-level command protocol (MCP) provides meaningful abstraction and automation over a plain CLI, and when the simplicity and direct control of a CLI wins. It emphasizes tradeoffs like readability, scripting, and ecosystem maturity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论在何种场景下更高层的命令协议 MCP 能带来对比简单 CLI 更有意义的抽象与自动化，以及何时应偏好 CLI 的简单性与直接控制。强调可读性、脚本化与生态成熟度等权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47266084' data-tags='Security,Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/politics/national-security/pentagon-formally-labels-anthropic-supply-chain-risk-escalating-conflict-ebdf0523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pentagon formally labels Anthropic supply-chain risk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>五角大楼正式将 Anthropic 供应链风险列为重点关注</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47266084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 429</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47266084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Pentagon formally flags Anthropic&#x27;s supply-chain risk, signaling heightened scrutiny of AI vendors used in defense contexts. The designation could affect contracts, compliance requirements, and risk mitigation measures across the defense AI ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>五角大楼正式将 Anthropic 的供应链风险列为重点关注对象，表明对用于防务领域的 AI 供应商将进行更严格审查。这一认定可能影响防务 AI 生态中的合同、合规要求与风险管理措施。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47176576' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.noelberry.ca/posts/making_games_in_2025/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making Video Games in 2025 (without an engine)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在2025年不用引擎也能做游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47176576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 423</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47176576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that solo developers can still ship modern games in 2025 without relying on traditional game engines, by leveraging modern tooling, custom pipelines, and lightweight frameworks. It discusses practical workflows, asset management, and performance considerations that enable indie developers to compete.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章论述在 2025 年，个人开发者无需传统引擎也能制作现代游戏，依赖现代工具、定制管线与轻量级框架。内容聚焦实际工作流、资源管理与性能考量，展示独立开发者的可行路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://noelberry.ca/posts/making_games_in_2025/postcard.png' data-full='https://noelberry.ca/posts/making_games_in_2025/postcard.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/proton-mail-helped-fbi-unmask-anonymous-stop-cop-city-protestor/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Proton Mail Helped FBI Unmask Anonymous &#x27;Stop Cop City&#x27; Protester</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Proton Mail 曾帮助 FBI 识别匿名 Stop Cop City 抗议者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47267628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 421</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47267628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proton Mail reportedly assisted the FBI in identifying an anonymous Stop Cop City protester, drawing privacy concerns about metadata leakage and law-enforcement access. The case prompts renewed debate over encrypted services and user anonymity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Proton Mail 协助 FBI 识别了一名匿名 Stop Cop City 抗议者，引发对元数据暴露与执法机构访问的隐私担忧。此事促使人们重新讨论加密服务与用户匿名性的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.404media.co/content/images/size/w1200/2026/03/Stop_Cop_City.jpg' data-full='https://www.404media.co/content/images/size/w1200/2026/03/Stop_Cop_City.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47241817' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cly2m5e5ke4o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TikTok will not introduce end-to-end encryption, saying it makes users less safe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TikTok 不引入端到端加密，称这会让用户更不安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47241817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 418</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47241817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 415</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TikTok states it will not implement end-to-end encryption, arguing it could endanger users or complicate moderation, while privacy advocates push for stronger encryption. The stance underscores the ongoing tension between user safety, regulatory compliance, and privacy on popular social apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TikTok 表示不采用端对端加密，理由是可能危及用户安全或让内容审核更复杂。此立场凸显在流行社交应用中，隐私、合规与安全之间的持续博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/642d/live/9816c7c0-1723-11f1-9120-a910fc22c6ac.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/642d/live/9816c7c0-1723-11f1-9120-a910fc22c6ac.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
