<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/apple-introduces-the-new-macbook-air-with-m5/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MacBook Air with M5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>搭载 M5 的 MacBook Air</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 415</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 501</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils a new MacBook Air powered by the M5, signaling a lightweight yet capable option for portable work with improved CPU/GPU performance and efficiency. Expect better battery life and a thinner, fanless or quiet design, depending on the model.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出配备 M5 的新款 MacBook Air，强调轻薄便携与更高效的性能。芯片升级带来更长续航和更强的处理能力，机身设计也可能更安静。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/apple-introduces-the-new-macbook-air-with-m5/tile/Apple-MacBook-Air-hero-260303-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603031721' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/apple-introduces-the-new-macbook-air-with-m5/tile/Apple-MacBook-Air-hero-260303-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603031721' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47301085' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://agent-safehouse.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agent Safehouse – macOS-native sandboxing for local agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Agent Safehouse：macOS 原生本地代理沙箱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47301085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47301085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agent Safehouse provides macOS-native sandboxing for local agents, letting autonomous tasks run in isolated environments without container overhead. By evading full containers while maintaining isolation, it reduces cross-agent risk and strengthens security for macOS automation workflows. This approach matters for developers deploying agents on Mac workstations or in Mac-centric stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Agent Safehouse 提供了 macOS 原生的本地代理沙箱化，允许自主任务在相对独立的环境中运行而无需容器开销。通过在不牺牲隔离的前提下提高安全性，它降低了跨代理的风险，提升了 macOS 自动化工作流的安全性。对于在 Mac 环境中部署代理的开发者尤为相关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://agent-safehouse.dev/og-image.png' data-full='https://agent-safehouse.dev/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47286311' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ki-editor.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ki Editor - an editor that operates on the AST</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ki Editor：一个在 AST 上工作的编辑器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47286311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 405</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47286311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ki Editor operates on the AST, letting you transform code by editing its syntax tree rather than raw text. This enables safer refactors and more reliable transformations across languages. It signals a shift toward AST-driven editing as a core editor capability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ki Editor 通过对 AST 进行操作来变更代码，而非直接编辑文本。这使重构更安全、跨语言的变换更可靠，体现了以代码结构为核心的编辑器发展方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ki-editor.org/img/tree-seal-script.svg' data-full='https://ki-editor.org/img/tree-seal-script.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47206824' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ivanturkovic.com/2026/02/25/ai-made-writing-code-easier-engineering-harder/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Made Writing Code Easier. It Made Being an Engineer Harder</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 让写代码更容易，却让工程师更难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 396</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI reduces the burden of writing code but shifts engineering work toward maintaining, testing, and integrating AI-generated artifacts. It raises expectations and reshapes skills and team workflows in nontrivial ways.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能降低了编写代码的门槛，但将工程工作的重点转向维护、测试与集成 AI 产物。它提高了期望值，改变了技能需求和团队的工作方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Relies on relicensing with AI-assisted rewrite to change licensing terms, exploring feasibility, ethical concerns, and legal implications. It looks at how AI tools can expedite licensing tasks, while prompting debate about originality and rights.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.3 Instant</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 公布 GPT‑5.3 Instant，面向开发者的快速响应与更高效部署。更新的重点在于降低延迟、优化成本，以及对流式与长提示的更好处理。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47248341' data-tags='Science,Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.experimental-history.com/p/the-one-science-reform-we-can-all' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Government grant-funded research should not be published in for-profit journals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>政府资助研究不应在营利性期刊发表</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47248341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 390</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47248341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A column argues that publicly funded science should be published in non-profit or open venues rather than for-profit journals. It discusses how paywalls and profit motives can distort access and research dissemination. This stance aligns with ongoing debates about scientific publishing reform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇专栏认为政府资助的科研应在非营利性或开放渠道发表，而非营利性期刊。文章指出付费墙与利润动机可能扭曲信息获取与研究传播，与当前关于科学出版改革的讨论相呼应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!OkWt!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01ba4c49-a417-4d02-8502-aef829fd1e42_1765x1167.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!OkWt!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01ba4c49-a417-4d02-8502-aef829fd1e42_1765x1167.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47232727' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WebMCP offers a browser-based preview of the MCP framework, signaling an accessible path for web-centric tooling and automation. Early previews hint at how developers might use modular APIs in the browser.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WebMCP 提供了基于浏览器的 MCP 框架预览，展示了面向网页工具与自动化的可访问路径。早期预览揭示了开发者在浏览器中使用模块化 API 的潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zipcodefirst.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Put the zip code first</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把邮编放在首位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47292485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 354</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47292485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates reorganizing data workflows to prioritize ZIP codes, improving geolocation accuracy, routing, and regional targeting in logistics and commerce. The approach surface-level argues for locality-aware design and could affect privacy as ZIP-level data becomes more central.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>倡导在数据工作流中将 ZIP 码放在首位，以提升定位、路由和区域定向的精度，改善物流与商业运营。此思路强调面向地理邻近性的设计，同时也可能带来更强的隐私考量。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47215565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 354</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47215565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 333</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>National labs adopt policies to limit foreign scientists&#x27; access amid security concerns, potentially hindering collaboration and talent pipelines in research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国家实验室将限制外国科学家进入实验室的政策，出于安全考虑。可能打击国际合作与人才流动，对科研生态与资助产生深远影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mlumiste.com/general/openai-charter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The changing goalposts of AGI and timelines</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AGI 时间线在变，未来仍未定</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47299009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 350</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47299009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 300</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AGI timelines are slippery because definitions and expectations shift; it urges clearer criteria and governance considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，AGI 的时间线因定义与预期的不断变化而难以把握，呼吁对标准化评估和治理的更清晰框架。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://health.ucdavis.edu/news/headlines/first-ever-in-utero-stem-cell-therapy-for-fetal-spina-bifida-repair-is-safe-study-finds/2026/02' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>First in-utero stem cell therapy for fetal spina bifida repair is safe: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>胎儿期脊柱裂修复的首例体内干细胞治疗显示安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study reports in-utero stem cell therapy for fetal spina bifida appears safe, marking a promising prenatal treatment for a serious condition. Long-term efficacy remains to be seen, but this advances prenatal regenerative medicine.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示胎内干细胞治疗用于修复胎儿脊柱裂的安全性初步结果良好，标志着产前干细胞治疗的潜力。未来需要关注长期效果和伦理议题。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47289311' data-tags='DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/research/a-decade-of-docker-containers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A decade of Docker containers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>十年 Docker 容器的发展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47289311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47289311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Docker popularized containerization and made portable environments, reproducible builds, and scalable deployments standard in modern software. Over a decade, tooling and orchestration matured, but security, performance, and complexity trade-offs persist.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Docker 让容器化成为软件交付的基石，推动了可移植环境、可重复构建与可扩展部署的常态化。十年来，工具链与编排生态不断完善，但在隔离、性能与复杂性之间的权衡仍然存在。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The tweaks illustrate how browser customization can dramatically affect daily productivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供通过 about:config 优化 Firefox 右键菜单体验的实用技巧，包括精简项、启用有用的快捷方式、恢复缺失项目等。此类自定义演示了浏览器设置对日常生产力的显著影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Helix 作为后现代文本编辑器，提供现代化的编辑体验、强大语言工具和可定制工作流，挑战传统编辑器的格局。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47268391' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/labor-market-impacts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Labor market impacts of AI: A new measure and early evidence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 对劳动力市场影响的新指标与初步证据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47268391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47268391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 545</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic introduces a new metric to quantify AI-related labor market effects and presents early findings showing mixed signals: some roles gain efficiency and resilience, while routine tasks face displacement. Implications for policy and workforce training are discussed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 引入新指标来量化 AI 对劳动力市场的影响，并给出初步证据，结果呈现两极：部分岗位提升效率与韧性，常规任务存在被替代的风险。对政策与职业培训具有重要启示。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47291292' data-tags='Science,Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/0x0mer/CasNum' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CasNum</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CasNum（化学 CAS 编号工具）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CasNum is a small GitHub project by 0x0mer focused on CAS numbers used in chemistry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CasNum 是 0x0mer 在 GitHub 上的一个小型项目，专注于化学物质的 CAS 编号。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.merkley.senate.gov/merkley-klobuchar-launch-new-effort-to-ban-federal-elected-officials-profiting-from-prediction-markets/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Effort to prevent government officials from engaging in prediction markets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阻止政府官员参与预测市场的努力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lawmakers push to ban federal elected officials from profiting in prediction markets to curb conflicts of interest and insider-trading risk. The move reflects growing scrutiny of how predictive markets intersect with governance and policy making.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>立法者推动禁止联邦民选官员在预测市场获利，以遏制利益冲突与内幕交易风险。这一举措反映出对预测市场在治理和政策制定中的作用日益审慎的态度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.merkley.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/Press-Release-Photo-4.jpeg' data-full='https://www.merkley.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/Press-Release-Photo-4.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedaily.com/releases/2026/02/260228093456.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New iron nanomaterial wipes out cancer cells without harming healthy tissue</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新型铁纳米材料可在不伤害健康组织的情况下清除癌细胞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47207404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47207404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new iron-based nanomaterial selectively targets and destroys cancer cells while sparing healthy tissue, pointing toward targeted therapies with fewer side effects. Clinical translation will require extensive safety validation and regulatory steps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种新型铁基纳米材料能选择性地攻击并清除癌细胞，同时尽量避免损伤健康组织，显示出副作用较低的靶向治疗潜力。进入临床需要全面的安全性评估与监管批准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/cancer-cell-self-destructing.webp' data-full='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/cancer-cell-self-destructing.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A public cost-tracker tracks the financial and human toll of Iran-related conflicts, aggregating sanctions, military spending, and civilian impact. It provides a data-driven lens on the economic burden of regional conflicts, useful for policymakers and technologists tracking macro-trends.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblog.ecuadors.net/cloud-vm-benchmarks-2026-performance-price-1i1m.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloud VM benchmarks 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 年云虚拟机基准：7 家提供商的 44 种实例性能与性价比</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47293119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47293119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An expansive benchmark compares 44 VM types across seven cloud providers, highlighting how performance per dollar varies across workloads from compute-heavy to memory-intensive. The findings help engineers choose cost-efficient instances and reveal that value is highly provider- and region-dependent, so the cheapest option is rarely best.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项涵盖七家云厂商、44种实例的基准测试，揭示不同工作负载下的性价比差异。研究帮助开发者在成本与性能之间做出取舍，并暴露出不同提供商与区域带来的价值差异，通常最便宜的选项并非最佳选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1000,height=420,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fnfspjoxt6h8z1l1tjprz.jpg' data-full='https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1000,height=420,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fnfspjoxt6h8z1l1tjprz.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mullvad VPN 在伦敦街头进行了一场挑衅性的广告行动，强调隐私保护并挑战传统广告规范。此举凸显以隐私为核心的品牌如何利用非常规手段触达受众。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47236958' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/pc-components/cpus/intels-make-or-break-18a-process-node-debuts-for-data-center-with-288-core-xeon-6-cpu-multi-chip-monster-sports-12-channels-of-ddr5-8000-foveros-direct-3d-packaging-tech' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intel&#x27;s make-or-break 18A process node debuts for data center with 288-core Xeon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英特尔 18A 制程首次用于数据中心，配备 288 核 Xeon</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47236958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47236958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Intel debuts its 18A process node for data centers with a 288-core Xeon multi-core monster, promising advances in density and efficiency with Foveros packaging and DDR5 capabilities. The launch matters for AI inference and HPC workloads, signaling how next-gen nodes aim to boost total cost of ownership for hyperscale customers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英特尔推出面向数据中心的 18A 制程节点，配备 288 核 Xeon，承诺在密度与能效方面带来突破，并结合 Foveros 封装与 DDR5。此举对 AI 推理与高性能计算工作负载意义重大，或降低大型云环境的总拥有成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/RMA9vvkBcjersBWv6qrbvd-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/RMA9vvkBcjersBWv6qrbvd-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47234917' data-tags='AI,DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://leodemoura.github.io/blog/2026/02/28/when-ai-writes-the-worlds-software.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When AI writes the software, who verifies it?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当 AI 编写软件，谁来验证它？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47234917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47234917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues verification remains the hard part when AI writes software; human audits, formal methods, and reliable pipelines are needed to ensure correctness, security, and compliance. It discusses governance and the risk of relying solely on AI for critical code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为当 AI 编写软件时，验证仍是最难的环节，需要人工审查、形式化方法与可靠的开发流程来确保正确性、安 全与合规，并讨论治理与对 AI 产出的过度信任风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://leodemoura.github.io/images/leo.jpg' data-full='https://leodemoura.github.io/images/leo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.documentfoundation.org/blog/2026/02/04/libreoffice-26-2-is-here/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LibreOffice Writer now supports Markdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LibreOffice Writer 现支持 Markdown</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47298885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47298885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LibreOffice Writer now supports Markdown, allowing users to write in Markdown and export to rich formats. This bridges traditional word processing with lightweight markup workflows, benefiting developers and technical writers who blend prose and code. It may influence how teams adopt LibreOffice for collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LibreOffice Writer 现已支持 Markdown，允许用户使用 Markdown 编写并导出为富文本格式。此举将传统文字处理与轻量级标记工作流连接起来，方便开发者和技术写作者在文本与代码之间切换。可能改变团队在协作中对 LibreOffice 的采用方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.documentfoundation.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/LO_26_2-light.png' data-full='https://blog.documentfoundation.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/LO_26_2-light.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://406.fail/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A standard protocol to handle and discard low-effort, AI-Generated pull requests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>处理和丢弃低 Effort、AI 生成拉取请求的标准协议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47267947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47267947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A standard protocol outlines how to handle and discard low-effort AI-generated pull requests, including labeling, human review thresholds, and criteria for auto-closure. The rule aims to keep codebases sane while leveraging AI assistance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一套标准协议规定如何处理和丢弃低质量的 AI 生成拉取请求，涵盖标签、人工审核门槛及自动关闭的条件，旨在在利用 AI 辅助的同时维持代码库的健康性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47291876' data-tags='Data,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qZuR-772cks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microscopes can see video on a laserdisc</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>显微镜也能读出激光光盘上的视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quirky demonstration shows microscopic observation can reveal the video encoded on a LaserDisc by analyzing the physical grooves. The stunt illustrates how data can be recovered from old media and invites reflection on data preservation and analog-to-digital interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的演示显示，通过观察激光光盘的物理凹槽，显微镜可以还原其中的视频信息。这提醒人们关注旧媒体的数据保存与模拟到数字接口的转化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9849.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RFC 9849. TLS Encrypted Client Hello</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TLS Encrypted Client Hello（RFC 9849）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47244291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 295</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47244291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains RFC 9849, which encrypts the TLS ClientHello to protect metadata during handshake from passive observers. It discusses deployment considerations, compatibility, and potential performance impacts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 RFC 9849，它在 TLS 握手阶段对 ClientHello 进行加密，以保护元数据不被被动监听者获取。讨论部署注意事项、兼容性以及潜在的性能影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.norn-labs.com/blog/huginn-report-feb-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Safe Browsing missed 84% of confirmed phishing sites</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Safe Browsing 未能拦截84%的经证实钓鱼站点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47262347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 294</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47262347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security report finds Google Safe Browsing blocked only a minority of confirmed phishing sites, leaving many attackers unblocked. The result underscores the limitations of browser-level protections and the need for additional phishing defenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全报告显示 Google Safe Browsing 未阻挡大量经证实的钓鱼站点，暴露浏览器层防护的局限性，并强调需要额外的钓鱼防护措施。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.moss.town/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moss is a pixel canvas where every brush is a tiny program</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Moss：每一笔刷都是小程序的像素画布</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Moss turns brush strokes into small programs, forming a pixel canvas. The design lets artists craft complex visuals by composing tiny algorithms, blurring the line between art and software. It showcases computation embedded directly in visual creation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Moss 将笔触变为小程序，形成像素画布。通过把微型算法拼接来创作复杂视觉效果，模糊了艺术与软件的边界。展示了将计算直接嵌入视觉创作中的创新做法。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/moongate-community/moongatev2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Moongate – Ultima Online server emulator in .NET 10 with Lua scripting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Moongate——用 .NET 10 与 Lua 脚本的 Ultima Online 服务器模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 281</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Moongate is a modern Ultima Online server emulator built in .NET 10 with Lua scripting, illustrating how legacy games can be reimagined with contemporary tooling. The project showcases rapid iteration, portability, and community-driven game hosting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Moongate 是用 .NET 10 和 Lua 脚本构建的现代版 Ultima Online 服务器模拟器，展示了用现代工具重现经典游戏的可能性。它体现了社区驱动的游戏托管、快速迭代和可移植性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ad0a16d38d2f8a3803ce7d34dbb65504ae08b8c76bfcf9deaa9fe2354a3bba8a/moongate-community/moongatev2' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ad0a16d38d2f8a3803ce7d34dbb65504ae08b8c76bfcf9deaa9fe2354a3bba8a/moongate-community/moongatev2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将大模型大小与系统内存、CPU、显卡匹配</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47211830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 280</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47211830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Practical guidance on sizing and deploying LLMs to fit hardware constraints, including model selection, quantization, memory management, and offloading strategies.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://libresprite.github.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LibreSprite – open-source pixel art editor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LibreSprite：开源像素画编辑器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47272799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47272799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LibreSprite is an open-source pixel art editor aimed at indie developers and hobbyists, offering sprite sheets, animation, and tilemap support. It lowers barriers to retro game creation and highlights the vitality of OSS tools for artists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LibreSprite 是一个开源像素画编辑器，面向独立开发者和爱好者，提供精灵动画、图块和工具链。它为独立游戏开发提供了易用且可扩展的工具。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://libre.solar' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Libre Solar – Open Hardware for Renewable Energy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Libre Solar：面向可再生能源的开源硬件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 272</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Libre Solar offers open hardware designs for renewable energy, from solar charge controllers to microinverters, enabling DIY communities to build and customize sustainable electronics. The project underscores the value of open-source hardware in accelerating energy tech adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Libre Solar 提供面向可再生能源的开源硬件设计，从太阳能充电控制器到微型逆变器，帮助社区搭建并定制可持续电子设备。该项目凸显了开源硬件在推动能源科技普及方面的价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://libre.solar/images/libresolar-logo-website-dark.png' data-full='https://libre.solar/images/libresolar-logo-website-dark.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47239042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 272</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47239042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Voxile showcases a ray-traced game built on its own engine and a custom programming language, illustrating how bespoke tech stacks can deliver tight performance and experimental tooling. The project highlights novelty in game development where engine and language are co-designed for specific art and performance goals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Voxile 展示了一款用自家引擎与自研编程语言实现的光线追踪游戏，体现定制化技术栈在性能和开发灵活性上的潜力。该项目突显引擎与语言协同设计在实现特定美术与性能目标中的创新。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.documentfoundation.org/blog/2026/03/05/cra-guidances/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LibreOffice: Request to the European Commission to adhere to its own guidances</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Document Foundation calls on the European Commission to follow its own guidelines, likely relating to compatibility, openness, or procurement standards. The note argues that adherence matters for interoperability, trust, and governance of open-source software in public sector adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文档基金会呼吁欧盟委员会遵循其自身指南，涉及兼容性、开放标准或采购规范等领域。文章强调遵循指南对公部门采用开源软件时的互操作性与信任治理具有关键作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fivetran.com/blog/anthropic-please-make-a-new-slack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic, please make a new Slack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic，请做一个新的 Slack</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47280200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47280200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing that AI orgs deserve a dedicated, privacy-conscious collaboration tool better suited for safety workflows than popular chat platforms. The piece riffs on how tooling choices shape collaboration, security, and incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章呼吁为 AI 组织打造一个隐私友好、比现有聊天工具更合适的内部协作工具，强调工具选择对安全、协作和事件响应的影响。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47243069' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/robertcprice/nCPU' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A CPU that runs entirely on GPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个完全在 GPU 上运行的 CPU</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47243069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 258</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47243069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A CPU implemented to run entirely on GPU hardware demonstrates offloading instruction scheduling and execution to GPU kernels, blurring the line between CPU and GPU architectures. The project is not about replacing CPUs yet, but it shows how GPU-centric designs could inform emulation, education, and certain HPC workloads. It hints at potential benefits like unified memory usage and high parallelism for specific tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个在GPU上完整运行的CPU展示了将指令执行移植到GPU内核的可能性，模糊了CPU与GPU的边界。该项目尚不能替代实际CPU，但为仿真、教育和高性能计算场景提供了新的设计思路，可能带来内存整合和并行性提升的潜在收益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b214ca809dd3d9caade6189aaaaf38bd75a9743f7f2acc98a0066e0b71de8714/robertcprice/nCPU' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b214ca809dd3d9caade6189aaaaf38bd75a9743f7f2acc98a0066e0b71de8714/robertcprice/nCPU' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47239943' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://raymyers.org/post/dont-make-me-talk-to-your-chatbot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t make me talk to your chatbot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别让我和你的聊天机器人对话</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47239943' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47239943' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of chatbots as customer-facing interfaces, arguing that automated assistants often degrade UX or misalign with user needs. The piece advocates for better design choices, including optional human fallback and transparent limitations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对以聊天机器人为前端的用户体验提出批评，认为自动化助手常常无法完全满足需求。主张更完善的设计，例如提供人类支持的后备选项与对能力边界的透明化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://raymyers.org/weblog-card-1.png' data-full='https://raymyers.org/weblog-card-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47275152' data-tags='Health,Science,Security,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lumafield.com/scan-of-the-month/health-wearables' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CT Scans of Health Wearables</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>健康穿戴设备的 CT 扫描</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lumafield 的本月扫描展示了健康穿戴设备的内部结构，如电池布局和传感器设计，关注安全性、可维修性与硬件工程细节。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国政府 iPhone 黑客工具包可能落入外国间谍与犯罪分子之手</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47237692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47237692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wired reports on a coercive cyber toolkit used by government intelligence that could fall into foreign spies or criminals&#x27; hands, raising concerns about supply chain, weaponization of iOS exploits, and export controls. The piece warns of consequences for user privacy and national security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wired 报道称一款来自政府情报部门的网络工具包可能流入外国间谍与犯罪分子之手，引发对供应链、iOS 漏洞的武器化及出口管制的担忧。此事对用户隐私与国家安全的潜在影响需关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arcanenibble.github.io/dumping-lego-nxt-firmware-off-of-an-existing-brick.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dumping Lego NXT firmware off of an existing brick (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从现有乐高 NXT 砖块提取固件（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47271988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47271988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate how to dump the firmware from an existing LEGO NXT brick. The write-up covers reading flash memory and bypassing protections to enable analysis, customization, or custom firmware. It underscores how hardware hacking of educational devices can illuminate both openness and security risks in consumer IoT.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者演示如何从现有的乐高 NXT 块中提取固件。文章介绍读取闪存和绕过保护以实现分析、定制或自定义固件的过程。此类硬件黑客行为凸显教育用设备的开放性与消费类物联网的安全风险。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 232</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Atlantic piece argues that war prediction markets create security risks and information hazards, potentially enabling manipulation or misleading signals in critical policy decisions. It underscores governance questions around open predictive markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为战争预测市场带来安全风险和信息隐患，可能导致在关键政策决策中产生误导性信号或被操纵，引发对开放预测市场治理的思考。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个低成本项目演示如何用廉价设备复刻宝丽来风格的照片。通过将便宜的相机或手机与自制相框和即时打印流程结合，实现在不花大钱的情况下获得复古效果。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47242765' data-tags='Design,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terriblesoftware.org/2026/03/03/nobody-gets-promoted-for-simplicity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobody gets promoted for simplicity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没人因为简单而升职</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47242765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 229</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47242765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Another take on the same thesis, elaborating how teams often equate simplicity with risk, and why leadership should reward clean, maintainable systems for sustainable progress.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对同一论题的另一种解读，阐述团队常把简单等同于风险，强调领导层应当奖励干净、可维护的系统，以实现可持续的进展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/terriblesoftware.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/chjpdmf0zs9sci9pbwfnzxmvd2vic2l0zs8ymdiylta1l25zmtexndetaw1hz2uta3d2d3b1c3kuanbn.webp?fit=1024%2C680&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/terriblesoftware.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/chjpdmf0zs9sci9pbwfnzxmvd2vic2l0zs8ymdiylta1l25zmtexndetaw1hz2uta3d2d3b1c3kuanbn.webp?fit=1024%2C680&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://growtika.com/blog/tech-media-collapse' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most-read tech publications have lost over half their Google traffic since 2024</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自 2024 年以来，大多数科技出版物的 Google 流量下降超过一半</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tech publications have lost over half of their Google traffic since 2024, signaling a seismic shift in how audiences discover content and the pressure on publishers to diversify referrals, subscription models, and direct visits. The trend underscores the importance of strong brand signals, newsletters, and alternative distribution channels.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自 2024 年以来，主流科技媒体的 Google 流量下降超过一半，标志着受众发现内容方式的重大转变，也给出版商带来多元化流量来源、订阅模式和直接访问的压力。此趋势强调强化品牌信号、邮件通讯与其他分发渠道的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://growtika.com/images/blog/tech-media-collapse-featured.webp' data-full='https://growtika.com/images/blog/tech-media-collapse-featured.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/03/amd-ryzen-ai-400-cpus-will-bring-upgraded-graphics-to-socket-am5-desktops/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AMD will bring its “Ryzen AI” processors to standard desktop PCs for first time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD 将把 Ryzen AI 处理器首次带入主流桌面 PC</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47216825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47216825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 203</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD will bring Ryzen AI CPUs to mainstream AM5 desktop PCs for the first time, introducing AI acceleration and upgraded graphics to consumer hardware. This move signals a broader industry push to bake AI capabilities into standard PCs, affecting software optimization and gaming workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 将首次把 Ryzen AI 处理器带入主流 AM5 桌面电脑，集成 AI 加速和改进的显卡性能到消费级硬件中。这一举措标志着厂商在消费级电脑中普遍嵌入 AI 能力的趋势，它将影响软件优化和游戏等工作负载的性能表现。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47288533' data-tags='Health,Science,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/flash-radiotherapy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FLASH radiotherapy&#x27;s bold approach to cancer treatment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FLASH 放射治疗：癌症治疗的大胆新途径</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47288533' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47288533' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FLASH radiotherapy delivers ultra-high-dose-rate beams to tumors, potentially reducing damage to surrounding healthy tissue and enabling higher tumor doses. Early clinical work shows promise but requires new equipment, protocols, and careful dose tracking; if scalable, it could redefine radiation oncology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FLASH 放射治疗以极高剂量率照射肿瘤，可能降低对周围健康组织的损伤并允许对肿瘤加大剂量。早期临床研究显示希望，但需新的设备、剂量学与操作流程；若普及，或将重新定义放射治疗领域。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=65111419&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C261%2C0%2C261' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=65111419&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C261%2C0%2C261' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This tool removes safety constraints from open-weight LLMs, enabling unrestricted generation but raising serious safety and abuse concerns. It underscores the tension between openness and responsible deployment of powerful models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个去除开源权重 LLM 审查的工具提高了生成自由度，但也带来严重安全与滥用担忧，凸显开放性与负责任部署之间的张力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/galileo-s-handwritten-notes-found-ancient-astronomy-text' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Galileo&#x27;s handwritten notes found in ancient astronomy text</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cache of Galileo&#x27;s handwritten notes embedded in an old astronomy text offers new clues about early scientific practice. The find could refine our understanding of how scientists recorded observations and ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在一部古老天文文本中发现了伽利略的手写笔记，为理解早期科学实践提供了新线索。此发现或有助于修正科学家记录观测与想法的方式的认识。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencealert.com/tinnitus-is-somehow-connected-to-a-crucial-bodily-function' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tinnitus Is Connected to Sleep</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>耳鸣与睡眠相关</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47287763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47287763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings link tinnitus severity to sleep quality and circadian biology, suggesting improving sleep may ease tinnitus symptoms. The connection hints at broader brain-body regulation affecting perception.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现耳鸣与睡眠质量及昼夜节律有联系，改善睡眠可能缓解耳鸣症状。这一关联揭示了大脑与身体在感知体验上的更广泛调控。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>二月份就业数据显示就业市场迅速收缩，消费者支出与美联储政策预期可能随之调整。科技行业的招聘节奏与初创融资也可能受波及。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://copyrightlately.com/mondrian-public-domain-controversy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mondrian Entered the Public Domain. The Estate Disagrees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蒙德里安进入公有领域，遗产方存在异议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mondrian&#x27;s works have fallen into the public domain, but the estate challenges that status, highlighting tensions around copyright boundaries for classic art. The dispute illustrates how legal interpretations of public-domain timing and rights can complicate reuse and licensing for museums, educators, and tech platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蒙德里安的作品已进入公有领域，但遗产方对这一状态提出异议，凸显了经典艺术版权边界的紧张。此争议揭示了公有领域起始时间与权利界定在再利用、展览和数字化应用中的复杂性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newrepublic.com/post/207381/kristi-noem-explain-company-ad-campaign' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Noem Can&#x27;t Explain Why She Hired 8-Day-Old Company for Ad Campaign</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>诺姆 无法解释为何为广告活动雇用一家8天前成立的新公司</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47259303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47259303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kristi Noem is criticized for hiring a company only eight days old for a campaign, raising questions about vendor due diligence and payroll politics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Noem 围绕其竞选活动雇佣一家成立仅8天的新公司引发质疑，凸显对供应商尽职调查和潜在利益冲突的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.newrepublic.com/3577f1fd296e2de47c3cb74b090d6e1c16339ddd.jpeg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;crop=faces&amp;fit=crop&amp;fm=jpg' data-full='https://images.newrepublic.com/3577f1fd296e2de47c3cb74b090d6e1c16339ddd.jpeg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;crop=faces&amp;fit=crop&amp;fm=jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/policy/887678/supreme-court-ai-art-copyright' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI-generated art can’t be copyrighted after Supreme Court declines review</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 生成的艺术在最高法院拒绝复审后不能获得版权</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 188</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Supreme Court declines to review whether AI-generated art can be copyrighted, effectively leaving the rule that AI-created works cannot be copyrighted in place. This decision shapes IP strategy for artists and platforms navigating AI-assisted workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最高法院拒绝就 AI 生成艺术的版权问题再审，维持现行规则：AI 创作的作品通常不能获得版权保护。这对艺术家与在 AI 辅助创作中运作的平台的知识产权策略具有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25301215/STK463_SCOTUS_B.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C9.9676601489831%2C100%2C80.064679702034&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25301215/STK463_SCOTUS_B.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C9.9676601489831%2C100%2C80.064679702034&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joelchrono.xyz/blog/setting-up-phones-is-a-nightmare/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Setting up phones is a nightmare</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>设置新手机是一场噩梦</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47170958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47170958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 221</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Onboarding modern smartphones is often painful, with data migrations, sign-ins, and ecosystem lock-in slowing setup. The post catalogs friction points and suggests pragmatic improvements—better data portability, unified sign-in, and clearer transfer tooling. Reducing friction here could improve satisfaction for device makers and carriers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代智能手机的初始设置常常让人倍感痛苦，数据迁移、账户登录和生态体系绑定等环节拖慢了整个设置过程。文章列出主要痛点，并建议改进方向，如更好的数据可移植性、统一登录与更清晰的迁移工具。减少这些摩擦有望提升用户满意度并惠及设备制造商与运营商。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://joelchrono.xyz/assets/img/banner.png' data-full='https://joelchrono.xyz/assets/img/banner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

It weighs the trade-offs between speed, weight, and ride comfort, offering practical guidance for real-world range gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇关于长续航电动自行车的文章聚焦通过增大电池、高效传动与重量控制来提升续航。它权衡了速度、重量与骑行舒适度，并给出实现实际续航提升的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/mar/04/us-tech-companies-energy-cost-pledge-white-house' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US tech firms pledge at White House to bear costs of energy for datacenters</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国科技公司在白宫承诺承担数据中心能源成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47256545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47256545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 237</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US tech firms pledge to bear energy costs for data centers, aligning with climate and energy policy goals. The move could shift operating expenses and influence where new facilities are located.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多家美国科技公司承诺承担数据中心能源成本，回应气候与能源政策目标。这一承诺可能改变运营成本结构，并影响新设施的选址与投资。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thetravel.com/tsa-leaves-passenger-needing-surgery-after-illegally-forcing-her-through-scanner-spinal-cord-implant/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TSA leaves passenger needing surgery after illegally forcing her through scanner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TSA 在安检中违规强制让乘客通过扫描仪，致其需要手术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47279189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47279189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investigative report alleges TSA agents improperly forced a passenger with a spinal implant through a scanner, causing injury and prompting questions about screening safety and patient rights. It spotlights the tension between security protocols and individual health risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有报道指 TSA 不当强行让携带脊柱植入物的乘客通过安检扫描，造成伤害并引发对安检安全与乘客权利的质疑。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/3/5/theseus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI and the Ship of Theseus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 与忒修斯之船</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 182</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay uses the Ship of Theseus metaphor to explore AI systems that replace components over time, raising questions about identity, reliability, and governance of evolving AI stacks. It invites reflection on what makes a system &#x27;the same&#x27; when its parts continually change.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文用忒修斯之船的比喻探讨随着时间更换组件的 AI 系统，提出身份、可靠性与治理的问题，思考在部件持续变化时何谓“同一系统”。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-03-05-theseus-social.png' data-full='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-03-05-theseus-social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opencamera.org.uk/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open Camera is a FOSS Camera App for Android</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Open Camera：一个面向 Android 的开源相机应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47219462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47219462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open Camera is a fully free and open-source camera app for Android, offering manual controls, RAW image support, and privacy-friendly design. Its FOSS nature makes it a strong alternative to vendor bloatware and closed ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Open Camera 是一个面向 Android 的开源相机应用，提供手动控制、RAW 支持等功能，注重隐私与可定制性。作为免费开源软件，它为抵制厂商臃肿应用提供了有力选项。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://retractionwatch.com/2026/03/03/canadian-pediatric-society-journal-correction-case-reports-fictional-paediatrics-child-health/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Medical journal says the case reports it has published for 25 years are fiction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>医学期刊称过去25年的病例报道为虚构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47248511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47248511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A medical journal admits that the case reports published over 25 years were fictional, triggering a crisis of trust in peer review and raising questions about editorial processes. The revelation has wide implications for researchers and patients.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家医学期刊承认过去25年的病例报道是虚构的，引发对同行评审制度的信任危机，并对编辑流程产生质疑，影响研究者和患者的信任与透明度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>发往 Outlook.com 的邮件因故障或过度拦截规则被拒收，干扰用户沟通。此事件凸显后台策略或错误如何引发广泛的可靠性问题，以及加强监控的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47208341' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.esa.int/Applications/Connectivity_and_Secure_Communications/World-first_gigabit-per-second_laser_link_between_aircraft_and_geostationary_satellite' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>World-first gigabit laser link between aircraft and geostationary satellite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球首个飞机与地球同步卫星之间的千兆位激光链路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47208341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47208341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ESA reports world-first gigabit laser link between aircraft and geostationary satellite, signaling new possibilities for airborne-to-space communications. Laser communications offer high throughput and low latency, but face alignment and weather challenges as the technology scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲航天局宣布实现全球首个空中与地球同步卫星之间的千兆位激光链路，标志着机载-太空通信的新前景。激光通信具有高吞吐、低时延等优势，但在对准与天气条件方面仍存在挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.esa.int/var/esa/storage/images/esa_multimedia/images/2026/02/aircraft_ready_to_test_airbus_ultraair_laser_terminal/27121761-2-eng-GB/Aircraft_ready_to_test_Airbus_UltraAir_laser_terminal_pillars.jpg' data-full='https://www.esa.int/var/esa/storage/images/esa_multimedia/images/2026/02/aircraft_ready_to_test_airbus_ultraair_laser_terminal/27121761-2-eng-GB/Aircraft_ready_to_test_Airbus_UltraAir_laser_terminal_pillars.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.replit.com/browsers-dont-want-to-be-cameras' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We Built a Video Rendering Engine by Lying to the Browser About What Time It Is</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们通过“欺骗浏览器时间”构建视频渲染引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47203827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47203827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The engineers describe a trick to control rendering cadence by manipulating perceived time from the browser, enabling custom synchronization or performance optimization. It raises questions about browser time semantics and potential risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过操控浏览器对时间的感知来控制渲染节奏，作者构建了一个视频渲染引擎。这一做法质疑浏览器时间API的局限性，同时也引发对可维护性与安全性的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>赫尔辛基实现整整一年零交通死亡，展示城市设计、交通安全政策与步行/自行车友好基础设施对降低致命车祸的作用。此成就为其他城市提供安全改进的参考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://understandlegacycode.com/blog/a-process-to-do-safe-changes-in-a-complex-codebase/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Use the Mikado Method to do safe changes in a complex codebase</a></p>
