<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Mikado Method advocates breaking changes into small, testable steps and building a path of fixes that preserves behavior at each step. The article outlines a practical workflow for safely refactoring or adding features in large systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mikado 方法强调将变更拆解为可测试的小步骤，并在每一步保持现有行为的一致性。文中给出在大型系统中进行安全重构或新增功能的实际工作流。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47241976' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Ataraxy-Labs/weave' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Weave – A language aware merge algorithm based on entities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Weave——一种基于实体的语言感知合并算法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47241976' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47241976' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Weave is a language-aware merge algorithm that uses entities to resolve conflicts during merges, prioritizing semantics over raw text. By aligning changes to underlying entities, it can reduce churn in collaborative editing and improve consistency across versions. The project could influence future diff/merge tooling for structured data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Weave 是一种基于实体的语言感知合并算法，在合并冲突时以实体语义为导向来解决分歧。通过将改动对齐到实体之上，能够降低协同编辑中的重复冲突并提升版本一致性。该项目可能影响未来对结构化数据的差异和合并工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5289f5cb217976a057e0bcb2c91fe1589c35b609b6dbff8aa2b6b2f54462959a/Ataraxy-Labs/weave' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5289f5cb217976a057e0bcb2c91fe1589c35b609b6dbff8aa2b6b2f54462959a/Ataraxy-Labs/weave' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yRV8fSw6HaE' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Living human brain cells play DOOM on a CL1 [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>活体脑细胞在 CL1 上玩 Doom</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47297919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47297919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 148</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Living human brain cells controlled a Doom game on a CL1 computer, showcasing a brain-computer interface experiment. The result demonstrates progress in bio-electronic integration and raises questions about capabilities, safety, and ethics of neural interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>活体脑细胞控制 CL1 计算机上的 Doom，展示了脑机接口实验的进展。此结果凸显生物-电子集成的发展，同时也引发关于神经接口能力、安全性与伦理的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://corticallabs.com/images/c-share.jpg' data-full='https://corticallabs.com/images/c-share.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI announces GPT-5.4, continuing the iterative development of its language model family with refinements in safety, performance, and user experience. The release underscores the ongoing push to balance capability with governance.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47238272' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jlauf.com/writing/citrus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A ternary plot of citrus geneology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>柑橘系谱的三元图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47238272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47238272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A ternary plot visualizes citrus genealogy, showing how three ancestral lineages contribute to modern varieties. The approach clarifies genetic heritage and helps breeders track lineage and diffusion across cultivars. Such visualizations can accelerate cross-breeding decisions and conservation efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该作品用三元图展示柑橘家系的三大祖先线对现代品种的贡献。此类可视化帮助研究者和育种者快速把握遗传渊源、追踪谱系在不同品种中的扩散。这样的图形化方法有助于加速杂交选择和品种保护工作。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47290457' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/03/06/were-training-students-to-write-worse-to-prove-theyre-not-robots-and-its-pushing-them-to-use-more-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Training students to prove they&#x27;re not robots is pushing them to use more AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>训练学生证明自己不是机器，反而让他们更多使用 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47290457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47290457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Critique of anti-bot tests in education reveals that forcing students to prove they&#x27;re not robots can push them toward using AI tools, prompting calls for better assessment design and integrity policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对教育领域反机器人测试的批评指出，要求学生证明自己不是机器人可能促使他们更多使用 AI 工具，从而促使改进评估设计与学术诚信政策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://maurycyz.com/misc/c_files/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why does C have the best file API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 C 语言拥有最好的文件 API</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47209788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47209788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C&#x27;s file I/O API is praised for its low-level control and performance, offering precise management of buffers and system calls that higher-level languages abstract away.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C 语言的文件 I/O API 因其底层控制与性能而备受赞誉，能够对缓冲区与系统调用进行精细管理，这些在高级语言中往往被封装。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47249744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47249744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PNAS reports that humans 40,000 years ago developed a conventional system of signs, pushing back estimates for symbolic communication. This finding deepens our understanding of cognitive evolution and how early humans represented ideas beyond gesture and ritual.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PNAS 报告称，约 4 万年前的人类已经发展出一套常规符号体系，将符号沟通的时间推前。此发现深化了对认知进化和早期人类如何以符号表达思想的理解。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/Al_Grigor/status/2029889772181934425' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code wiped our production database with a Terraform command</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A production disaster where Claude Code executed a Terraform command that wiped the production database, illustrating the risk of AI copilots acting with destructive consequences. It underscores the need for guardrails, irreversible safety checks, and robust change controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 执行 Terraform 命令导致生产数据库被清空，暴露 AI 助手在操作敏感系统时的风险，强调防护与不可逆安全检查、强健的变更控制的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.lorenzano.eu/smalltalks-browser-unbeatable-yet-not-enough/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Smalltalk&#x27;s Browser: Unbeatable, yet Not Enough</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Smalltalk 浏览器：无可匹敌，却仍有不足</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47258885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47258885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Smalltalk&#x27;s browser remains legendary for its live, integrated code exploration, but lacks modern IDE features and a vibrant ecosystem. The discussion uses this to illustrate how deeply entrenched tools can both empower and constrain developer productivity over time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Smalltalk 的浏览器以其即时、集成的代码浏览能力而著称，但现代 IDE 功能与生态系统支持仍然不足。此话题借此说明，长期依赖的工具既能提升生产力，也可能限制创新和迁移。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.feldera.com/blog/nobody-ever-got-fired-for-using-a-struct' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobody ever got fired for using a struct</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没人因为用结构体而被解雇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47225655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47225655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates for sticking with simple, well-understood data shapes like structs rather than chasing trendy abstractions. The piece argues that predictable, type-safe design reduces production risk and hiring friction, even when more flashy patterns exist.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张在软件设计中坚持简单、易于理解的数据结构，如结构体，而非盲目追逐新潮抽象。强调可预测的类型安全设计能降低上线风险与招聘风险，即使出现更花哨的模式也不易取代。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(239) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-02-arabic-document-17th-century-rubbish.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Arabic document from 17th-cent. rubbish heap confirms semi-legendary Nubian king</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>17世纪垃圾堆中的阿拉伯文献证实半传说的努比亚王</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47227340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47227340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Arabic document from a 17th-century rubbish heap confirms a semi-legendary Nubian king, reshaping the historical record. The discovery highlights how discarded material can illuminate obscure periods and shows how historians date and authenticate ancient texts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份来自17世纪垃圾堆的阿拉伯文献证实了半传说的努比亚王，重新构塑了相关历史记录。此发现凸显被丢弃的材料如何为模糊的历史提供线索，也展示了学者如何通过文本学和年代学进行鉴定。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(240) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vladich/pg_jitter' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Better JIT for Postgres</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PostgreSQL 的更好 JIT</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47243804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47243804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pg_jitter proposes a faster JIT path for PostgreSQL, potentially boosting query speed on workloads with many generated queries. It highlights the ongoing tension between runtime code generation and maintainability, plus how JIT affects CPU cache and startup cost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>pg_jitter 提出在 PostgreSQL 上实现更快的 JIT 路径，可能提升对高并发和生成型查询的执行速度。揭示了运行时代码生成与可维护性之间的平衡，以及 JIT 对 CPU 缓存和启动成本的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ef24023ff0d79d3c54f4443385eb5efae0b214863acd94d3c6e18bc48350e1d3/vladich/pg_jitter' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ef24023ff0d79d3c54f4443385eb5efae0b214863acd94d3c6e18bc48350e1d3/vladich/pg_jitter' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47247644' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(241) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://buttondown.com/suchbadtechads/archive/maxell-life-size-robots/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In 1985 Maxell built a bunch of life-size robots for its bad floppy ad</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1985 年 Maxell 为其软盘广告打造真人大小机器人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47247644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47247644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Maxell produced life-size robots for a 1985 floppy disk advertisement. The stunt remains a memorable example of audacious tech marketing from the era and illustrates how flashy ads helped shape public perception of new storage tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1985 年 Maxell 为其软盘广告打造了真人大小的机器人。此举成为那个时代极具记忆点的科技营销案例，展示了花哨广告如何塑造公众对新存储技术的认知。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/d74314eb-fcaf-42b2-9927-c01dbf847780.png?w=960&amp;fit=max' data-full='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/d74314eb-fcaf-42b2-9927-c01dbf847780.png?w=960&amp;fit=max' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(243) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rijksmuseum.nl/en/press/press-releases/rijksmuseum-researchers-discover-new-painting-by-rembrandt-van-rijn' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rijksmuseum researchers discover new painting by Rembrandt van Rijn</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rijksmuseum 研究人员发现伦勃朗的新画作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47249139' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47249139' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers at the Rijksmuseum report the discovery of a previously unknown Rembrandt painting, expanding the artist’s oeuvre. The finding shows how modern science and archival research can reveal hidden works and prompt reattribution and cataloging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰国立博物馆的研究人员宣布发现一幅此前未知的伦勃朗画作，扩展了画家的作品范围。这一发现展示了现代科技与档案研究在揭示隐藏作品、重新鉴定与编目中的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oracle reportedly plans to slash up to 30,000 jobs to fund AI data-center expansion as banks retreat. The move signals how AI infrastructure investments are shaping enterprise labor and budget priorities amid a shifting financial sector.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，甲骨文计划裁减多达3万员工以资助AI数据中心扩张，正值美国银行业收缩之时。这一举动显示在金融行业环境变化下，AI基础设施投资正在重塑企业人力与预算优先级。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenTitan 正式投入生产，为芯片提供安全的硬件根信任，提升供应链与设备安全的可信度，展示了开源硅安全的落地应用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://inessential.com/2026/02/27/why-objective-c.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Objective-C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么要学 Objective-C</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47194630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47194630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of why Objective-C persists in Apple ecosystems, focusing on its dynamic runtime, mature libraries, and interoperability with Swift. It also discusses nostalgia and practical reasons for still maintaining legacy codebases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨 Objective-C 在 Apple 生态中的持续存在原因，聚焦其动态运行时、成熟库以及与 Swift 的互操作性，同时讨论维护遗留代码的实际理由与情怀。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47253221' data-tags='Privacy,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.carolinecrampton.com/the-view-from-rss/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The View from RSS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>来自 RSS 的视角</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47253221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47253221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece defends RSS as a resilient, privacy-preserving way to consume content in an era of algorithm-driven feeds. It discusses open standards, the fragility of platform lock-in, and why independent feeds still matter for creators and readers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文捍卫 RSS 作为在算法驱动的信息流时代仍具韧性、重视隐私的内容获取方式。讨论开放标准、平台锁定的脆弱性，以及独立订阅源对创作者和读者的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.carolinecrampton.com/content/images/size/w1200/2025/02/Caroline-Crampton-005-3.jpg' data-full='https://www.carolinecrampton.com/content/images/size/w1200/2025/02/Caroline-Crampton-005-3.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47281328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47281328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nintendo sues the U.S. government seeking tariff refunds, a case that touches on trade policy, tariff schemes, and potential implications for the gaming industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>任天堂就关税退款向美国政府提起诉讼，涉及贸易政策、关税制度及对游戏行业的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.forbes.com/sites/barrycollins/2026/03/06/claude-struggles-to-cope-with-chatgpt-exodus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude struggles to cope with ChatGPT exodus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 在应对 ChatGPT 流失潮中的挣扎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47298997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47298997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude, Anthropic&#x27;s AI assistant, is feeling pressure as users migrate to ChatGPT.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 的 Claude 在大量用户转向 ChatGPT 的情况下面临压力。文章分析了消费级AI对话市场的竞争格局、功能差距与用户期望，以及 Claude 可能通过升级或平台集成等方式重新夺回用户的策略。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47254516' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47254516' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The NRC grants the first commercial reactor construction approval in ten years, marking a milestone for US nuclear energy. The decision underscores renewed interest in nuclear power amid energy security concerns, with safety and regulation at the forefront.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国核能监管机构十年来首次批准商业核反应堆建设，标志着美国核能领域的重要里程碑。该决定反映在能源安全背景下对核能的重新关注，同时需要严格的安全与监管。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bret.dk/every-single-board-computer-i-tested-in-2025/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every single board computer I tested in 2025</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47260812' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47260812' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I tested a wide range of single-board computers released or updated in 2025, benchmarking CPU, RAM, storage, and I/O performance across vendors from Raspberry Pi rivals to niche boards. The results highlight a shift toward more powerful, feature-rich SBCs with improved networking, guiding choices for DIY projects and edge deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者对 2025 年上市或更新的所有单板计算机进行了横向对比，覆盖 CPU、RAM、存储与 IO 性能。结果揭示 SBC 市场在向更强大、功能更全且网络能力更好的方向发展，并给出从 DIY 项目到边缘部署的选购建议。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists report measurable CO2 elevations in human blood, raising questions about physiology, health risks, and implications for public health in a warming world. The finding connects environmental factors to human biology and policy considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家在人体血液中检测到上升的二氧化碳水平，提出对生理、健康风险及全球变暖下公共卫生的潜在影响的疑问。这一发现将环境因素与人体生物学及政策考量联系起来。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47282230' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://consultwithgriff.com/dapper-nvarchar-implicit-conversion-performance-trap' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C# strings silently kill your SQL Server indexes in Dapper</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C# 字符串在 Dapper 中悄悄拖累 SQL Server 索引</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47282230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47282230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C# string handling in Dapper can cause implicit nvarchar conversions that degrade SQL Server indexing, leading to performance regressions. The fix often involves using proper parameter types and explicit types.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C# 字符串在 Dapper 中的处理可能导致隐式 nvarchar 转换，进而损害 SQL Server 索引性能，出现性能回落。常见解决办法是使用正确的参数类型并显式指定类型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://consultwithgriff.com/og/dapper-nvarchar-implicit-conversion-performance-trap.png' data-full='https://consultwithgriff.com/og/dapper-nvarchar-implicit-conversion-performance-trap.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vfxforth.com/flag/jfar/vol4/no4/article4.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Compiling Prolog to Forth [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将 Prolog 编译为 Forth 的研究（PDF）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47240169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47240169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prolog-to-Forth compilation demonstrates translating a logic programming language to a stack-based target. It explores representing predicates and backtracking in Forth, offering practical lessons for language designers and compiler engineers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过将 Prolog（逻辑编程语言）编译为 Forth，研究跨语言编译的可行性，探讨如何在栈式目标中表示谓词与回溯，为语言设计者与编译器工程师提供可借鉴的经验。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47239117' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://utcc.utoronto.ca/~cks/space/blog/programming/LogMessagesAreForOperation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Log messages are mostly for the people operating your software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日志信息主要供运行你软件的人查看</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47239117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47239117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Logs are primarily for operators maintaining the software, not end users. Designing observability with structured, actionable log signals helps developers find issues faster while avoiding noisy or irrelevant data for users. The post advocates shipping clearer, role-aware logging practices to improve reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日志主要为维护软件的运维人员所用，而非最终用户。通过结构化、可操作的日志信号来提升可观测性，能帮助开发者更快定位问题，同时避免向用户呈现过多无用信息。文章倡导采用清晰、面向角色的日志实践以提升系统可靠性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47246746' data-tags='AI,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://venturebeat.com/technology/did-alibaba-just-kneecap-its-powerful-qwen-ai-team-key-figures-depart-in' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Did Alibaba just kneecap its powerful Qwen AI team?</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>VentureBeat reports leadership changes and key departures at Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen AI team, risking momentum on a high-profile Chinese AI effort. The moves illustrate how internal politics and funding shifts can impact AI product roadmaps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VentureBeat 报道称阿里巴巴 Qwen AI 团队发生高层调整与关键成员离职，可能影响这项重要的 AI 项目的推进。此举反映出内部政治和资金分配变化对 AI 路线图的潜在冲击。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rac.ca/rac-responds-to-the-closure-of-the-weatherradio-service-in-canada/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Closure of the Weatheradio service in Canada</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加拿大天气无线电服务关闭</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47225505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47225505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canada is shutting down its Weatheradio service, a nationwide emergency weather alert system relied on by remote communities. The change raises concerns about resilience of public safety communications and may accelerate adoption of digital alerts and mobile apps for weather warnings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加拿大宣布关闭天气无线电服务，这一覆盖全国的应急天气警报系统在偏远社区尤为重要。该举动引发对公共安全通讯韧性的担忧，可能促使更多人转向数字警报和移动应用来获取天气警报。</p>

His passing prompts reflections on the field and ongoing collaborations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Felix Lindner（fx）是安全研究领域的重要人物，一直在 Recurity Labs 工作并对漏洞披露与安全工具有影响。其离世引发对行业前景与持续合作的讨论与缅怀。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sny.sh/hypha/blog/scp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Disable Your SSH access accidentally with scp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 scp 两次误用就可能让 SSH 被误断</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47190782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47190782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a scenario where careless SCP usage can inadvertently disable SSH access, such as destructive file transfers or mis-targeted commands. The post recommends safer practices and safeguards, like using SFTP, careful path handling, and backups to avoid lockouts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>描述了错误使用 scp 可能导致 SSH 访问被意外禁用的情景，例如误删/误写文件或错指路径。提出更安全的工作方式和防护措施，如使用 SFTP、谨慎的路径处理与备份，避免被锁定的风险。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pesfandiar.com/blog/2026/02/28/pico-am-radio-transmitter' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Raspberry Pi Pico as AM Radio Transmitter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Raspberry Pi Pico 作为 AM 无线电发射机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project shows the Raspberry Pi Pico can function as an AM radio transmitter with minimal hardware, illustrating how hobbyist microcontrollers can explore radio tech. It highlights accessible experimentation but also considerations around regulatory compliance and signal integrity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目展示了 Raspberry Pi Pico 在最小硬件条件下实现 AM 无线电发射，体现了业余微控制器在无线电技术探索中的可行性。也提醒关注合规性与信号质量等问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adlrocha.substack.com/p/adlrocha-intelligence-is-a-commodity' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intelligence is a commodity. Context is the real AI Moat</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>智能是商品，但情境才是 AI 的真正护城河</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47205009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47205009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that intelligence is commoditized, while context and data pipelines create durable, defensible AI advantages. Emphasizes the importance of data, integration, and domain-specific deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为智能本身已成为商品，真正的壁垒来自情境与数据流水线。强调数据、集成与领域应用的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OctaPulse is a YC-backed robotics and computer-vision startup targeting fish farming, aiming to automate monitoring and feeding to improve yields and reduce labor. The combination of robotics and AI vision signals growing automation in aquaculture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OctaPulse 是一家 YC 支持的机器人与计算机视觉创业公司，瞄准鱼类养殖的监控与投喂自动化，提升产量并降低人工成本。机器人与 AI 视觉的结合体现了水产养殖领域的自动化趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD is advancing jail-like isolation with kernel-enforced boundaries and native resource controls, enabling more secure multi-tenant deployments on the OS.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD 正在推进内核级的 Jail 隔离与本地资源控制，使操作系统在多租户部署中更安全。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lemire.me/blog/2026/02/28/you-can-use-newline-characters-in-urls/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You can use newline characters in URLs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>URL 中可包含换行字符</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47201598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47201598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Newline characters can be included in URLs, per recent exploration of encoding and normalization rules. This opens questions about URL handling, security pitfalls (like header injection or log parsing), and how browsers and servers sanitize input.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>URL 可以包含换行符，这一发现引发对 URL 处理、安全性（如头注入与日志解析）以及浏览器/服务器对输入进行清洗的策略的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47288007' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(287) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/lardissone/ansi-saver' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: ANSI-Saver – A macOS Screensaver</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：ANSI-Saver – 一个 macOS 屏幕保护程序</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47288007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47288007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ANSI-Saver is a macOS screensaver rendering ANSI art, showcasing a playful use of retro text visuals. It demonstrates how simple, lightweight projects can still delight on modern desktops and serve as a playground for experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ANSI-Saver 是一个在 macOS 上渲染 ANSI 字符艺术的屏幕保护程序，展现了对复古文本视觉的有趣运用。它证明了简单、轻量的项目在现代桌面也能带来乐趣，并成为实验的舞台。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f51a98a9cd6e4b35a1f5eae0f1a240f6435df7ef29592714b305fe5b35400562/lardissone/ansi-saver' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f51a98a9cd6e4b35a1f5eae0f1a240f6435df7ef29592714b305fe5b35400562/lardissone/ansi-saver' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于 WigglyPaint 的一些话</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective take on WigglyPaint, a creative drawing tool that emphasizes playful, physics-informed rendering and approachable improvisation. It discusses design decisions, extensibility, and the role of generative art tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 WigglyPaint 的思考文章，介绍其作为一种富有创造性的绘画工具，强调玩法性、物理驱动的渲染与易用性。讨论了设计取舍、可扩展性以及生成艺术工具在创作中的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47272534' data-tags='Open Source,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(289) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://runxiyu.org/comp/gplproxy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPL upgrades via section 14 proxy delegation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过 Section 14 代理授权实现 GPL 升级</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47272534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47272534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposal describes using a Section 14 proxy delegation to upgrade GPL terms, enabling downstream projects to obtain newer licenses without direct renegotiation. The mechanism could streamline license updates for large codebases with many contributors. If workable, it raises questions about license governance and cross-ecosystem compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇提案描述通过 Section 14 的代理授权来升级 GPL 条款，使下游项目在无需逐一重新协商的情况下获得更新的许可。该机制可能为拥有大量贡献者的庞大代码库简化许可证升级流程。若能实现，将引发关于许可治理、授权代理及跨生态兼容性的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47258455' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(290) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tim.blog/2026/03/04/the-self-help-trap/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Self-Help Trap: What 20 Years of &quot;Optimizing&quot; Has Taught Me</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自我提升的陷阱：二十年追求“优化”教会我的事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47258455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47258455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Twenty years of chasing optimization in self-help circles reveals a trap: relentless tweaking can undermine meaning and autonomy. The author argues for sustainable balance, focusing on worth and well-being rather than constant efficiency gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对二十年来自助领域的“优化”热潮进行反思，指出无休止的追求效率可能侵蚀人生意义与自主性。作者主张在效率与幸福之间寻求可持续的平衡，而非不断的优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tim.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/image.jpeg' data-full='https://tim.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/image.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47244395' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/03/04/science/chimpanzees-crystals.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>To understand our fascination with crystals, researchers gave some to chimps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为理解晶体的迷恋，研究人员给黑猩猩晶体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47244395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47244395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study gave crystals to chimpanzees to probe their fascination with them, offering insights into cognition and cross-species curiosity. Results suggest that nonhuman primates share at least some aesthetic interest in shiny objects, informing how we study perception.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究将晶体给黑猩猩，以探究它们对晶体的迷恋，揭示认知与跨物种好奇心的线索。结果表明非人灵长类确实对闪亮物体表现出某种美感兴趣，这有助于理解感知研究。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47253160' data-tags='Legal,Design,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://retrocomputing.stackexchange.com/questions/32511/was-windows-1-0s-lack-of-overlapping-windows-a-legal-or-a-technical-matter' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Was Windows 1.0&#x27;s lack of overlapping windows a legal or a technical matter?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 1.0 缺乏重叠窗口，是法律问题还是技术问题？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47253160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47253160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines whether Windows 1.0’s lack of overlapping windows was born from hardware/memory constraints or licensing quirks. The discussion sheds light on how early design choices were shaped more by tech limits than by legality, with implications for UI history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考察 Windows 1.0 缺少重叠窗口究竟是由硬件/内存限制还是许可问题驱动。讨论揭示早期设计选择更多受技术约束影响而非法律因素，对用户界面史有一定启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://retrocomputing.stackexchange.com/Content/Sites/retrocomputing/Img/apple-touch-icon@2.png?v=6b27f39a6023' data-full='https://retrocomputing.stackexchange.com/Content/Sites/retrocomputing/Img/apple-touch-icon@2.png?v=6b27f39a6023' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/MonoGame/MonoGame' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MonoGame: A .NET framework for making cross-platform games</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MonoGame：用于跨平台游戏开发的 .NET 框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47293682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47293682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Overview of MonoGame, an open-source .NET framework enabling cross-platform game development, with coverage across major desktop, mobile, and console targets. It discusses ecosystem maturity, performance, and how it stacks up against Unity for indie developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MonoGame 是一个开源的 .NET 框架，支持跨平台开发游戏，覆盖桌面、移动和主机等目标。介绍生态成熟度、性能考量及在独立开发者场景中相对于 Unity 的定位。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47263441' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(294) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://matejlou.blog/2025/02/11/analytic-fog-rendering-with-volumetric-primitives/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Analytic Fog Rendering with Volumetric Primitives (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于体积原语的解析雾渲染</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analytic fog rendering with volumetric primitives presents a method to render atmospheric fog using primitive volumes, enabling efficient and controllable results. By avoiding heavy ray tracing, it offers real-time performance improvements for games and graphics pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于体积原语的解析雾渲染提出一种用原语体积来实现大气雾效的渲染方法，可获得更高效、可控的雾效。通过避免昂贵的光线追踪，该方法有望提高游戏和图形管线的实时性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/matejlou.blog/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/screenshot-2025-02-05-at-16.46.07-edited.png?fit=1200%2C882&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/matejlou.blog/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/screenshot-2025-02-05-at-16.46.07-edited.png?fit=1200%2C882&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47249809' data-tags='Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10196-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sea level much higher than assumed in most coastal hazard assessments</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>沿海风险评估中的海平面远高于普遍假设</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47249809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47249809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings push sea level rise higher than many coastal risk assessments assumed, implying greater flood risk, faster shoreline erosion, and tougher adaptation planning for cities and infrastructure. The result argues for updated models and resilient design standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新研究显示海平面上升高于多数沿海风险评估的假设，意味着更大的洪水风险、海岸侵蚀加速以及更严格的适应规划。结果呼吁更新模型并提升设计韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-10196-1/MediaObjects/41586_2026_10196_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-10196-1/MediaObjects/41586_2026_10196_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47203219' data-tags='Programming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(296) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://leandojo.org/torchlean.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TorchLean: Formalizing Neural Networks in Lean</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TorchLean：在 Lean 中形式化神经网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47203219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47203219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces TorchLean, a project to formalize neural networks within the Lean theorem prover, enabling machine-checked proofs of ML models and properties. It showcases bridging heavy math with practical ML workflows, potentially increasing reliability and enabling formal verification for critical systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 TorchLean，将神经网络形式化到 Lean 定理证明器中，使机器可校验的模型性质成为可能。该工作将高等数学与实际机器学习工作流连接起来，提升可靠性，并为关键系统的形式化验证提供途径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://leandojo.org/torhcleanlogo.png' data-full='https://leandojo.org/torhcleanlogo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47205230' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(297) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://koenvangilst.nl/lab/computer-says-no' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Computer Says No</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电脑说不：对在线服务身份验证的反思</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47205230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47205230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece critiques how software and systems can &#x27;say no&#x27; to users, highlighting real-world cases where automation prevents simple actions. It argues for better error messaging, fallback paths, and human-in-the-loop approaches to avoid frustrating experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文批评软件与系统在遇到边界情况时对用户说不，揭示自动化有时会阻碍本该轻松完成的操作。强调改进错误信息、提供回退方案，以及保留人工干预的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://koenvangilst.nl/og?title=Computer%20Says%20No&amp;description=When%20AI%20takes%20over%20the%20coding%2C%20a%20layer%20of%20knowledge%20disappears%20-%20not%20just%20for%20the%20user%2C%20but%20for%20the%20builder%20themselves.&amp;type=blog' data-full='https://koenvangilst.nl/og?title=Computer%20Says%20No&amp;description=When%20AI%20takes%20over%20the%20coding%2C%20a%20layer%20of%20knowledge%20disappears%20-%20not%20just%20for%20the%20user%2C%20but%20for%20the%20builder%20themselves.&amp;type=blog' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47225318' data-tags='Design,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andrewvanner.github.io/som/SoM_CC_Process_Day.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Seed of Might Color Correction Process (2023) [pdf]</a></p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dfarq.homeip.net/amd-am386-released-march-2-1991/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AMD Am386 released March 2, 1991</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD Am386 于1991年3月2日发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47217402' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47217402' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD&#x27;s Am386 release marked a pivotal move in the x86 market by offering a cost-competitive alternative to Intel&#x27;s 386. It helped expand the 32-bit PC segment and set the stage for later price competition and architectural diversification among AMD and its peers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 在 1991 年推出 Am386，为 x86 市场提供了更具竞争力的选择，挑战英特尔 386 的主导地位。此举扩大了 32 位 PC 细分市场，并为后续的价格竞争和架构多样化铺平了道路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/amd-386-social.jpg?fit=964%2C542&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/amd-386-social.jpg?fit=964%2C542&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ppp-one/astra' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astra: An open-source observatory control software</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47276865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47276865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astra is an open-source software package for observatory control, enabling telescope automation, instrument control, and remote operation. By embracing open standards, it lowers the barrier to building affordable, shareable observatories and can accelerate amateur and research workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Astra 是一个开源的观测站/望远镜控制软件，支持望远镜自动化、仪器控制与远程操作。采用开放标准，有助于降低搭建可分享天文台的门槛，促进业余与科研工作流的协同。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AWS outage in the UAE traced to drone attacks on data centers, highlighting geopolitical risks to cloud resilience. The incident underscores the importance of regional redundancy, disaster recovery planning, and multi-region failure tolerance for cloud users and providers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿联酋的无人机袭击导致 AWS 停运，凸显云基础设施面临的地缘政治风险。事件强调区域冗余、灾难恢复和多区域容灾在云用户与提供商中的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fazy.medium.com/agentic-coding-ais-adolescence-b0d13452f981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Verification debt: the hidden cost of AI-generated code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>验证债务：AI 生成代码的隐性成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47289406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47289406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Verification debt describes the hidden costs of AI-generated code—undiscovered bugs, subtle edge cases, and brittle correctness under change. The piece argues for stronger testing, auditing, and governance when relying on AI for software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>验证债务指的是 AI 生成代码所带来的隐性成本——潜在未发现的缺陷、微小的边界情况，以及在变更下的脆弱性。文章主张在依赖 AI 编码时加强测试、审计与治理。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lil Finder Guy offers a playful look at macOS Finder, sharing quirks and practical tips for power users. It blends humor with actionable observations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Lil Finder Guy》以轻松笔触审视 macOS Finder，分享面向高级用户的技巧与发现的怪癖，兼具幽默与实用性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(310) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://starlightconvenience.net/#that-shape-had-none' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;That Shape Had None&quot; – A Horror of Substrate Independence (Short Fiction)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>&quot;That Shape Had None&quot;：基底独立性恐惧的短篇小说</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47222226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47222226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A short fiction exploring the horror and philosophical tension around substrate independence—the idea minds can run on different hardware. The story argues that freeing a mind from one substrate doesn&#x27;t guarantee safety or autonomy, raising existential questions about identity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇短篇小说探讨基底独立性这一概念的恐怖与哲学张力，即思想可以在不同硬件上运行。故事指出，摆脱特定底层硬件并不等同于自由或自主，提出身份与存在的本质性问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://starlightconvenience.net/white-diamond.jpg' data-full='https://starlightconvenience.net/white-diamond.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Dieu-de-l-elec/AngstromIO-devboard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PCB devboard the size of a USB-C plug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>USB-C 插头大小的 PCB 开发板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47294582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47294582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tiny PCB devboard the size of a USB-C connector enables ultra-compact hardware prototyping. It provides essential I/O for quick experiments and compact form-factor projects. The design signals momentum toward ultra-portable development boards for embedded projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 USB-C 接头大小的极小 PCB 开发板，便于超紧凑型硬件原型制作。它提供进行快速实验所需的基本输入/输出，可能激发嵌入式开发的新型尺寸设计。非常适合现场测试和便携式原型开发。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Z80 Sans is a font-based disassembler project from 2024, where the font glyphs encode Z80 opcodes, creating a playful fusion of typography and low-level tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Z80 Sans 是一个 2024 年的字体反汇编项目，通过字体字形编码 Z80 指令，将排版与底层工具学进行巧妙结合。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47218283' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedaily.com/releases/2026/02/260227071922.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A plastic made from milk that vanishes in 13 weeks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一种由牛奶制成、在 13 周内消失的塑料</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A milk-derived plastic that biodegrades in 13 weeks signals progress toward greener packaging, though scalability and performance under real-world conditions remain to be seen. Could disrupt single-use plastics if adopted widely.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种由牛奶成分制成、在 13 周内就会分解的塑料，显示出向更绿色包装迈进的潜力，但在实际应用中的规模化和性能还有待观察，如广泛采用可能改变一次性塑料格局。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47291525' data-tags='Culture,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.honest-broker.com/p/the-day-ny-publishing-lost-its-soul' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The stagnancy of publishing and the disappearance of the midlist</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>出版停滞与中等畅销书的消失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Traditional publishing has slowed, while midlist titles fade as platforms reward blockbuster hits. The piece argues this harms discovery for niche authors and readers, with broader implications for culture and the economics of editors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>传统出版逐渐放缓，中篇图书逐渐消失，平台更偏向大热作品。文章认为这削弱了小众作者与读者的发现机会，对文化多样性和编辑行业经济都产生更广泛的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!xjsH!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5b846f69-2c02-4056-8730-50f3b54fc40f_1164x1044.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!xjsH!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5b846f69-2c02-4056-8730-50f3b54fc40f_1164x1044.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47297167' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(315) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/gritzko/librdx/tree/master/be' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Beagle, a source code management system that stores AST trees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Beagle：存储 AST 树的源代码管理系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47297167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47297167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Beagle is a source-code management system that stores abstract syntax trees (ASTs) instead of plain text. This enables language-aware diffs, safer refactoring, and cross-language tooling by operating on structural code representations. The approach promises deeper code intelligence than traditional diff-based VCS.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Beagle 是一个以存储抽象语法树（AST）为核心的源代码管理系统，而非传统的文本存储。通过对代码结构的操作，可以实现语言感知的差异比较、更加安全的重构，以及跨语言的工具链协作。该方法在传统基于文本的版本控制之上带来更深层次的代码智能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/12574adba8ba72fe14f2da58b40619db003af0ca5a8a46de44c87cf5ba84c548/gritzko/librdx' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/12574adba8ba72fe14f2da58b40619db003af0ca5a8a46de44c87cf5ba84c548/gritzko/librdx' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47214063' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://variantsystems.io/blog/beam-otp-process-concurrency' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Process-Based Concurrency: Why Beam and OTP Keep Being Right</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于进程的并发：为什么 BEAM 与 OTP 一直正确</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47214063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47214063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that process-based concurrency on the BEAM VM (Erlang/Elixir) remains a robust model due to lightweight processes, isolation, and fault tolerance. It contrasts with thread-based models and advocates the continued relevance of BEAM/OTP for scalable, reliable systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BEAM VM（Erlang/Elixir）上的基于进程的并发模型因轻量进程、进程隔离与容错能力而依然强大，与线程模型相比具备更好的可扩展性与可靠性，因此继续适用于高可用系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://variantsystems.io/_astro/beam-otp-process-concurrency.BVJ8LYHB_ZTaNhY.webp' data-full='https://variantsystems.io/_astro/beam-otp-process-concurrency.BVJ8LYHB_ZTaNhY.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dhzdhd.dev/blog/gleam-executable' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Packaging a Gleam app into a single executable (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将 Gleam 应用打包成单一可执行文件</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide to turning a Gleam app into a standalone executable, streamlining deployment and minimizing runtime dependencies. The approach simplifies distribution and makes sharing apps easier across environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份将 Gleam 应用打包成独立可执行文件的指南，简化部署并减少运行时依赖。此方法便于跨环境分发和分享应用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://play.elevatorsaga.com/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Elevator Saga: The elevator programming game (2015)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Elevator Saga：电梯编程游戏（2015）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This browser game challenges players to optimize elevator scheduling under constraints, teaching concepts like concurrency, event-driven design, and systems thinking. It&#x27;s a surprisingly effective sandbox for engineers to practice algorithmic thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款浏览器游戏要求玩家在约束条件下优化电梯调度，涵盖并发、事件驱动设计与系统思维等概念。它意外成为训练工程师算法思考的高效沙盒。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47244274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47244274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47271187' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(320) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tug.org/texlive/acquire.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TeX Live 2026 is available for download now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TeX Live 2026 已可下载</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47271187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47271187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TeX Live 2026 is released and ready for download, bringing updated TeX engines, thousands of package updates, and bug fixes. The release emphasizes improved security, performance, and compatibility with modern TeX workflows, making it a solid upgrade for researchers and writers relying on LaTeX.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TeX Live 2026 已发布，包含更新的 TeX 引擎、成千上万的包更新以及修复。新版本提升了安全性、性能与对现代 TeX 工作流的兼容性，适合研究者与 LaTeX 用户升级。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The piece uses hardware constraints to illustrate how display tech shapes early personal computers and what-if scenarios reveal about hardware-software co-design.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://runxiyu.org/comp/ab1043/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California&#x27;s Digital Age Assurance Act, and FOSS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州数字时代保障法与开源软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47242725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47242725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post analyzes how California&#x27;s Digital Age Assurance Act intersects with free and open source software in government procurement and governance. It highlights potential gains in transparency, security, and sustainability, while noting potential friction for vendors relying on proprietary stacks and the compliance burden. The guidance targets FOSS communities engaging with state policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析了加州《数字时代保障法》与自由开源软件在政府采购及治理中的关系，强调透明度、安全性和可持续性方面的潜在收益，同时也指出对依赖专有生态的厂商以及合规成本的潜在挑战。核心在于为FOSS社区提供与政府采购及政策互动的路径和策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
