<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Payment processing fees can dramatically erode margins, especially for small businesses and developers who process many transactions. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>支付处理费对利润的侵蚀往往比想象中更严重，尤其是对小型企业和大量交易的开发者。文章量化各类费率的隐藏成本，并给出降低支出、谈判条款或优化结账流程等实用建议。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47183196' data-tags='Web,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(332) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.blogsareback.com/guides/enable-cors' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Enable CORS for Your Blog</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为你的博客启用 CORS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47183196' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47183196' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pragmatic guide to enabling CORS on a blog, outlining how to configure response headers to allow cross-origin asset access while avoiding common pitfalls that may introduce security risks. Includes tips on when to enable, what to expose, and how to audit the impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文提供在博客上启用 CORS 的实用指南，讲解如何配置响应头以允许跨域资源访问，同时避免常见的安全隐患。包括何时开启、暴露哪些信息以及如何评估影响。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47258169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47258169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Running OpenBSD on SGI hardware has proven challenging but rewarding, highlighting the fragility of porting modern OSes to aging platforms and the tenacity of the open-source community.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 SGI 硬件上运行 OpenBSD 的经历起伏明显，暴露出将现代操作系统移植到老旧平台的挑战，也体现了开源社区的韧性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨数据具有实际重量这一观念在现代存储中对成本和管理的影响，尤其在 SSD 上更为显著，从而影响系统设计与架构决策。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers reconstruct prehistoric European diets through experimental cooking and isotope-informed sourcing to understand ancient meals. The work highlights culinary diversity and techniques, shedding light on social practices and trade in the past.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者通过实验烹饪和同位素分析的饮食线索，重现史前欧洲人的饮食结构，探讨古代餐饮的多样性与技艺，以及社会组织与贸易网络的线索。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇1974年的里程碑式论文探讨了编程语言设计中的核心取舍，强调表达能力、简洁性与实用性的平衡。它提出的关于语言抽象、语法与语义的见解对现代语言设计仍具影响力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47294924' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ataraxy-labs/sem' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sem – Semantic version control. Entity-level diffs on top of Git</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sem：语义版本控制，在 Git 之上实现实体级差异</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47294924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47294924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sem introduces semantic version control on top of Git, using entity-level diffs to track changes beyond plain text. It aims to improve traceability, audits, and reproducibility for data- and structure-rich projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sem 在 Git 之上实现语义化版本控制，按实体级别记录差异，超越文本层面的变更。目标是提升对数据与结构丰富项目的可追溯性、审计与可复现性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d812be72624d3c1de1c7408e5ef4954abe060fe86fdc5d48620da682118a7a18/Ataraxy-Labs/sem' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d812be72624d3c1de1c7408e5ef4954abe060fe86fdc5d48620da682118a7a18/Ataraxy-Labs/sem' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47222777' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.crowdsupply.com/sutajio-kosagi/precursor/updates/xous-0-10-0-introducing-baochip-1x-support' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Xous security focused open source on 22nm custom silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>22nm 自定义硅工艺的 Xous 安全导向开源操作系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47222777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47222777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Xous is a security-focused open-source OS designed for a 22nm custom BaoChip silicon platform, with emphasis on a small trusted code base and formal security properties. The update introduces BaoChip 1.x support and related hardening features, illustrating the growing ecosystem of open-source firmware for bespoke hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Xous 是一个面向安全设计的开源操作系统，面向 22nm 定制 BaoChip 芯片，并强调小型可信代码库与形式化的安全属性。此次更新引入 BaoChip 1.x 支持与相关的加固特性，体现了为定制硬件构建的开源固件生态日益成熟。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47224832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47224832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A microbenchmark reveals a type hint operation with about 185 microseconds of overhead, illustrating how lightweight static typing can be in practice. The post argues for the feasible incorporation of type hints in performance-sensitive code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项微基准测试显示类型提示操作的开销约为185微秒，说明在实际场景中静态类型标注的成本可以很低。文章探讨在对性能敏感的代码中加入类型提示的可行性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47256923' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(365) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.inngest.com/blog/no-lost-updates-python-asyncio' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What Python’s asyncio primitives get wrong about shared state</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python 的 asyncio 原语在共享状态方面哪里错了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47256923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47256923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Python&#x27;s asyncio primitives can mishandle shared state, leading to subtle race conditions or lost updates in concurrent code. The piece surveys patterns that avoid these pitfalls, such as explicit synchronization, avoiding shared mutable state, and using higher-level abstractions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Python 的 asyncio 原语在处理共享状态时容易出错，可能在并发代码中出现细微的竞争条件或丢失更新。文章探讨了避免这些坑的模式，如显式同步、避免共享可变状态，以及使用更高层的抽象。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.inngest.com/assets/blog/no-lost-updates-python-asyncio/cover.png' data-full='https://www.inngest.com/assets/blog/no-lost-updates-python-asyncio/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shows how dynamic feature detection can enable faster C code by selecting optimized paths at run time, trading a bit of complexity for performance gains on diverse hardware. Includes practical notes on implementation pitfalls and portability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了动态特征检测如何通过在运行时选择优化路径来提升 C 代码性能，在不同硬件上换取性能提升，同时需权衡实现难度和可移植性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The LFortran project has added support to compile the Fortran Package Manager (fpm), signaling maturity of the Fortran ecosystem and easing dependency management for projects. This aligns performance-oriented scientific computing with modern tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LFortran 现已实现对 Fortran 包管理器 fpm 的编译支持，标志着 Fortran 生态系统的成熟并简化依赖管理。此举让面向科学计算的性能化工作流更易接入现代工具链。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47260028' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(368) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dynomight.net/bourdieu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bourdieu&#x27;s theory of taste: a grumbling abrégé (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>布迪厄的品味理论：简要概述</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47260028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47260028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The abridged view of Bourdieu’s theory distills habitus, cultural capital, and social distinction into digestible ideas. It explains how tastes signal class, shape exposure to opportunities, and influence cultural markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>简要梳理了布迪厄的习惯世界、文化资本与社会分层的核心思想，解释了品味如何标识阶层、影响机会获取与推动文化市场运作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dynomight.net/img/bourdieu/bm3.jpg' data-full='https://dynomight.net/img/bourdieu/bm3.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cowleyforniastudios.com/2026/03/08/announcing-eyot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Eyot, A programming language where the GPU is just another thread</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Eyot：GPU 只是另一条线程的编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47296355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47296355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Eyot treats the GPU as just another thread, offering a unified model for parallelism. The language design highlights how scheduling GPU work like regular threads could simplify certain compute tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Eyot 把 GPU 当作普通线程对待，提供统一的并行计算模型。其设计强调将 GPU 工作像普通线程一样调度，可能简化某些计算任务的实现。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflection on over-automation where processes are automated but insiders lose sight of what is actually happening, highlighting the need for better observability and human context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对过度自动化的反思——流程被自动化，却难以把握真实发生了什么，凸显提升可观测性与保留人类判断的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(374) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/03/llms-can-unmask-pseudonymous-users-at-scale-with-surprising-accuracy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs can unmask pseudonymous users at scale with surprising accuracy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型能在大规模上揭露伪匿名用户，精度出人意料</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47242453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47242453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LLMs can unmask pseudonymous users at scale with surprising accuracy</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究显示，LLMs 能在大规模范围内以出人意料的高准确率揭示伪匿名用户，利用文本风格和公开元数据等线索。此现象引发对隐私保护的重大担忧，并促使加强去标识化和相关治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/unmask-deanymize-privacy-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/unmask-deanymize-privacy-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>监控的平凡性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47289331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47289331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Surveillance is described as a banal, everyday feature of modern life, embedded in devices and apps. The piece urges readers to rethink privacy design and push for better safeguards and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>监控已成为现代生活中平凡且无处不在的一部分，嵌入在各类设备与应用中。文章呼吁重新审视隐私设计，并推动更完善的保护与透明度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Palus Finance (YC W26): Better yields on idle cash for startups, SMBs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN: Palus Finance（YC W26）：为初创和中小企业闲置现金提供更高收益</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Palus Finance aims to optimize yields on idle corporate cash for startups and SMBs, offering treasury-style products designed for small balances. Backed by YC&#x27;s W26 batch, it arrives as founders seek yield without sacrificing liquidity or safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Palus Finance 目标是为初创和中小企业的闲置现金提供更高的收益，像企业金库工具一样管理短期资金。来自 YC 第26期，面向小额余额的低风险、可流动性保障的收益方案，帮助企业提升现金回报。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://matklad.github.io/2026/02/25/against-query-based-compilers.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Against Query Based Compilers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反对基于查询的编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Matklad argues against query-based compilers, citing concerns about correctness, maintainability, and optimization potential in such pipelines. The post advocates more explicit, IR-driven designs and modular compilation stages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Matklad 反对基于查询的编译器，指出此类流水线在正确性、可维护性与优化潜力方面存在问题。文中倡导采用更明确的中间表示和模块化的编译阶段。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openstreetmap.org/user/FeetAndInches/diary/408268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Converting dash cam videos into Panoramax images</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将行车记录仪视频转换为 Panoramax 图像</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47215180' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47215180' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a workflow to convert dash-cam footage into Panoramax-style panorama images for mapping. The process involves frame extraction, stitching, and projection steps, enabling richer, near-real-time mapping coverage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍将行车记录仪视频转换成 Panoramax 风格全景图的工作流，涵盖帧提取、拼接与投影等步骤，从而提升地图覆盖与制图能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.openstreetmap.org/assets/osm_logo_256-ed028f90468224a272961c380ecee0cfb73b8048b34f4b4b204b7f0d1097875d.png' data-full='https://www.openstreetmap.org/assets/osm_logo_256-ed028f90468224a272961c380ecee0cfb73b8048b34f4b4b204b7f0d1097875d.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(382) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mistivia/lmp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lisp-style C++ template meta programming</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47292029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47292029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses using Lisp-like techniques in C++ template metaprogramming, leveraging functional styles and S-expression-inspired patterns to improve expressiveness and possibly reduce boilerplate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论在 C++ 模板元编程中应用 Lisp 风格的技巧，利用函数式风格和受 S 表达式启发的模式来提升表达力，或减轻样板代码。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modernillustration.org' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Modern Illustration: Archive of illustration from c.1950-1975</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现代插画：二十世纪中叶至1975年的插图档案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An online archive curates mid-20th-century illustration, offering designers a rich source of style references and historical context. The collection highlights how art history informs contemporary UI and branding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个在线档案整理了二十世纪中叶至 1975 年的插图资源，为设计师提供丰富的风格参考与历史背景。该收藏强调艺术史如何影响当代界面与品牌设计。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47218541' data-tags='Entertainment,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(384) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/02/28/nx-s1-5730373/georges-melies-robot-film-1897-library-of-congress-gugusse-automate' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>19th century silent film that first captured a robot attack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>19世纪首部记录机器人攻击的无声电影</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Georges Méliès’s late-19th-century silent film capturing a robot attack offers a rare glimpse into early cinema’s imagination of automation. It highlights how 1890s filmmakers serialized tech-driven fantasy and influenced later sci-fi storytelling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>乔治·梅里埃斯19世纪末的一部无声电影首次呈现机器人攻击，提供了对早期电影对自动化幻想的珍贵观察。影片展现了当时对科技题材的想象力，并影响了后来的科幻叙事。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/1036x583+0+96/resize/1400/quality/85/format/png/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F36%2F7a%2Ffdaefeca4a1e80008a131adb570d%2Fscreenshot-2026-02-28-at-12-53-04.png' data-full='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/1036x583+0+96/resize/1400/quality/85/format/png/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F36%2F7a%2Ffdaefeca4a1e80008a131adb570d%2Fscreenshot-2026-02-28-at-12-53-04.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47266736' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/fastino-ai/GLiNER2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLiNER2: Unified Schema-Based Information Extraction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLiNER2：统一的基于模式的信息抽取</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47266736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47266736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLiNER2 extends unified schema-based information extraction, offering a common framework for pulling structured data across domains. By anchoring extractions to shared schemas, it simplifies data integration and improves model interoperability. The project aims to reduce bespoke extraction work and accelerate downstream analytics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLiNER2 将统一的基于模式的信息抽取扩展到一个通用框架，支持跨领域提取结构化数据。通过将抽取结果锚定于共享的模式，简化数据集成并提高模型的互操作性。该项目旨在减少定制化抽取工作，加速后续分析流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e1a0e35d6fba98d9976f71b56ce776dafc801d7c429f1371caab4654d47283c0/fastino-ai/GLiNER2' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e1a0e35d6fba98d9976f71b56ce776dafc801d7c429f1371caab4654d47283c0/fastino-ai/GLiNER2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47264741' data-tags='AI,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47264741' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Vela (YC W26) – AI for complex scheduling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN: Vela（YC W26）— 复杂调度的 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47264741' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47264741' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vela uses AI to manage scheduling across complex calendars and constraints, reducing back-and-forth and improving coordination efficiency for teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vela 通过 AI 管理跨复杂日历和约束的调度，减少来回沟通、提升团队协调效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47242637' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(387) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Speculative Speculative Decoding (SSD)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>推测性解码（SSD）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47242637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47242637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Speculative Decoding speeds up autoregressive generation by using a fast draft decoder to propose tokens, with a larger model later verifying and finalizing them. The extended approach improves throughput while preserving quality, clarifying deployment considerations and trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文提出的推测性解码策略通过一个快速的草拟解码器先给出候选标记，再由更大模型进行验证和最终确认，以降低推理成本并尽量保持输出质量。扩展版本在吞吐量和质量之间权衡，讨论了在实际部署中的应用要点和潜在挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47235117' data-tags='Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nber.org/202603/digest/pass-through-tariffs-evidence-european-wine-imports' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pass-Through of Tariffs: Evidence from European Wine Imports</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关税的传导：来自欧洲葡萄酒进口的证据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47235117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47235117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An NBER digest shows tariff changes partially pass through to European wine prices, with varying pass-through across channels and time. The finding informs policy debates about tariff design, consumer impact, and supply chain dynamics in a regulated market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项 NBER 摘要显示关税变动对欧洲葡萄酒价格存在一定程度的传导效应，传导程度因渠道与时点而异。研究结果为关税设计、消费者影响以及供应链在受监管市场中的动态提供了证据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nber.org/sites/default/files/2022-06/NBER-FB-Share-Tile-1200.jpg' data-full='https://www.nber.org/sites/default/files/2022-06/NBER-FB-Share-Tile-1200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47301663' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.eu/article/switzerland-cash-right-constitution-vote/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Swiss vote places right to use cash in country&#x27;s constitution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞士公投将现金使用权写入宪法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47301663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47301663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Switzerland&#x27;s vote enshrines a constitutional right to use cash, shaping future debates on digital payments, privacy, and financial inclusion. The ruling could influence how retail, banking, and regulators balance cash and electronic transactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞士公投将现金支付权写入宪法，影响未来数字支付、隐私保护与金融包容性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.politico.eu/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=630,fit=crop,quality=80,onerror=redirect/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/08/GettyImages-2252836951-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.politico.eu/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=630,fit=crop,quality=80,onerror=redirect/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/08/GettyImages-2252836951-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47280550' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(390) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pbs.org/newshour/health/takes-elephant-amount-time-poop' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why it takes you and an elephant the same amount of time to poop (2017)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么你和大象排便需要同样的时间</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47280550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47280550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece explains that physiological transit time scales are dominated by gut dynamics, not body size, leading to surprisingly similar times across mammals. The piece uses this biology insight to illuminate how scaling laws shape everyday phenomena.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中解释排便时间并非随体型线性放大，而是受肠道动力学控制，因此多种哺乳动物的排便时间其实相近。以此为例，讨论尺度规律如何影响日常现象与生物学的实际意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d3i6fh83elv35t.cloudfront.net/newshour/app/uploads/2015/05/542106059-1024x683.jpg' data-full='https://d3i6fh83elv35t.cloudfront.net/newshour/app/uploads/2015/05/542106059-1024x683.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47218521' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(391) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://journals.aps.org/pre/abstract/10.1103/p19h-9ysx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Screeching Sound of Peeling Tape</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>剥离胶带时的刺耳声</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study analyzes why peeling tape emits a squeal, attributing it to stick-slip dynamics and peeling angle. It notes implications for material science and robotics grippers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该研究分析了撕开胶带时发出尖锐声响的原因，归因于粘滑动力学与撕离角度。研究结果对材料科学和机器人夹持等领域具有启示意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47236218' data-tags='AI,Business,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(392) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsletter.danielpaleka.com/p/you-are-going-to-get-priced-out-of' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You are going to get priced out of the best AI coding tools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你将被最好的 AI 编码工具的定价挡在外</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47236218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47236218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Leading AI-powered coding tools are moving beyond affordable tiers, raising the cost of benefiting from AI-assisted development. This pricing squeeze could push individuals and small teams toward open-source alternatives, longer on-prem solutions, or tighter usage to stay within budget. The trend matters because it directly affects developer productivity and tool accessibility in the AI era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>领先的 AI 编码工具定价正向更高的门槛迈进，影响个人开发者和小团队的使用成本。为维持成本效益，可能推动转向开源替代品、本地化方案或更高效的使用策略。这个趋势关系到开发者生产力和在 AI 时代对工具的可得性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Od3b!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd15db1d6-903d-42d8-9e7c-1a6a0a1f27bf_1800x1200.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Od3b!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd15db1d6-903d-42d8-9e7c-1a6a0a1f27bf_1800x1200.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47247030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47247030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates editing incremental changes using patch format with the Jujutsu tool, enabling precise version control for both text and binary changes. It illustrates practical workflows for patch-based collaboration and reversible edits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示如何使用 Jujutsu 工具在补丁格式中编辑增量改动，实现对文本与二进制变更的高精度版本控制，提供可操作的补丁协作与可回滚的工作流示例。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pianoterm maps shell commands to piano keys, letting users trigger terminal actions with a MIDI-like interface. It&#x27;s a playful, practical example of periphery-driven productivity and could inspire more creative tooling around command-line workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pianoterm 将 shell 命令映射到钢琴按键，使用户能够通过像 MIDI 一样的界面触发终端操作。这是对外设驱动生产力的一种趣味且实用的示例，或将激发更多命令行工作流的创意工具。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://justin.poehnelt.com/posts/rewrite-your-cli-for-ai-agents/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You need to rewrite your CLI for AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你需要为 AI 代理重写你的命令行界面</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47252459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47252459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Traditional CLIs often undercut the capabilities of AI agents due to rigid command structures. The post advocates rewriting a CLI to support state, orchestration, and structured prompts so AI agents can operate more reliably across multi-step tasks. It argues a modern CLI should separate interface from model, enabling better testing and composability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>传统的命令行界面对 AI 代理的能力造成限制，往往因结构僵硬而难以支撑多步任务。文章主张重写 CLI，加入状态管理、编排和结构化提示，使 AI 代理在多步任务中更可靠地工作。它强调将界面与模型解耦以提升可测试性与组合性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://justin.poehnelt.com/posts/rewrite-your-cli-for-ai-agents/og.png' data-full='https://justin.poehnelt.com/posts/rewrite-your-cli-for-ai-agents/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47302463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47302463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece reveals a quirky mechanism whereby /proc/self/mem can write to memory regions that appear unwritable, exposing a subtle bypass in memory protection. It underscores how Linux exposes process memory to tooling and the potential risks for security researchers and attackers. The takeaway is to be mindful of /proc exposure when hardening systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章揭示 /proc/self/mem 竟能写入看似不可写的内存区域，暴露了内存保护的微妙绕行。它强调 Linux 将进程内存暴露给工具的方式带来的潜在安全风险，既有利于研究也可能被利用。加固系统时应警惕对 /proc 的暴露。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/offlinemark.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/02/proc-blog-post2-v4.png?fit=1911%2C1438&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/offlinemark.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/02/proc-blog-post2-v4.png?fit=1911%2C1438&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codeberg.org/nwellnhof/libxml2-ee' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Libxml2 Enterprise Edition (AGPL, from the previous maintainer)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Libxml2 企业版（来自前维护者，采用 AGPL 许可）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47216541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47216541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Libxml2 Enterprise Edition is released under AGPL by the previous maintainer, raising licensing concerns for corporate users who rely on proprietary software with XML parsing. The AGPL terms require sharing improvements when used over network, which affects embedded and SaaS deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Libxml2 Enterprise Edition 由前维护者以 AGPL 许可发布，令依赖该库的企业用户对许可条款产生关注，因为 AGPL 在网络使用场景下要求公开源代码。此举对嵌入式、SaaS 部署及商业使用的合规性产生影响，文中讨论了许可含义与社区分叉的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://codeberg.org/nwellnhof/libxml2-ee/-/summary-card' data-full='https://codeberg.org/nwellnhof/libxml2-ee/-/summary-card' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(427) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://niketpatel.com/essays/why-go-cant-try' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Go Can&#x27;t Try</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 Go 不能 试错</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47218090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay arguing that Go&#x27;s error handling model rejects traditional exceptions, favoring explicit error checks and simple control flow. It discusses trade-offs and how this shapes Go&#x27;s ergonomics for practical programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇论述 Go 语言错误处理模型为何不采用 try/except 的文章，强调其通过显式错误检查与简单控制流来实现稳健性，同时也带来可用性上的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(431) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/lixiasky-back/coroTracer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I made a zero-copy coroutine tracer to find my scheduler&#x27;s lost wakeups</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：我做了一个零拷贝的协程跟踪器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I built a zero-copy coroutine tracer to detect missed wakeups in my scheduler, reducing the overhead of tracing while diagnosing latency issues. The approach helps pinpoint wakeup gaps without perturbing the system&#x27;s timing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我做了一个零拷贝的协程跟踪器，用来定位调度器中丢失的唤醒，从而在不增加额外开销的情况下诊断延迟问题。该方法在不打扰系统时序的前提下找到唤醒间隙。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(432) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://netflixtechblog.com/mount-mayhem-at-netflix-scaling-containers-on-modern-cpus-f3b09b68beac' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mount Mayhem at Netflix: Scaling Containers on Modern CPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Netflix 的 Mount Mayhem：在现代 CPU 上扩展容器规模</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Netflix discusses Mount Mayhem, a project tackling how to scale container workloads efficiently on newer CPUs, leveraging hardware features like large pages, memory bandwidth, and speculative execution considerations. The work highlights performance and efficiency gains for latency-sensitive streaming workloads and could inform best practices for large-scale container deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Netflix 介绍 Mount Mayhem 项目，研究在现代 CPU 上高效扩展容器负载，利用大页、内存带宽与推测执行等硬件特性来提升性能。该工作对面向低延迟流媒体的海量容器部署带来潜在的性能和能效提升，并可能为大规模部署提供最佳实践。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275157' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275157' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal account explaining how a production database crash increased AWS costs by 10% due to data recovery, downtime, and reconfiguration. It underscores the importance of backups, disaster recovery plans, and cost-aware incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享因意外删除生产数据库导致的成本上升（约 10%），归因于数据恢复与系统重建。强调备份、灾备计划与事件响应的成本与风险。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reviews the early home computer era, detailing the competition among major platforms and how cost, ecosystems, and developer communities shaped consumer adoption. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾早期家庭电脑时代，详述各大平台之间的竞争如何通过成本、生态系统与开发者社区影响了用户采用。突出关键里程碑，以及这场“战争”对平台锁定与标准化的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(447) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://evanhahn.com/the-two-kinds-of-error/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two kinds of error</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>错误的两种类型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47211955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47211955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes a taxonomy of errors in software: mistakes that arise from incorrect assumptions and mistakes that stem from ambiguous interfaces or brittle systems. Understanding these categories informs better debugging, testing, and resilient design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出对软件错误的两种基本分类型的分类：源于错误假设的失误，以及源自不稳定接口或脆弱系统的错误。理解这两类错误有助于改进调试、测试以及提升系统的鲁棒性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(453) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/can-wealthy-family-change-course-deadly-brain-disease' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can a wealthy family change the course of a deadly brain disease?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>富裕家族能改变致命脑病的进程吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47284061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47284061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Private philanthropy is funding aggressive brain-disease research, raising questions about how wealth can accelerate science while shaping research priorities. The piece weighs potential breakthroughs against risks of bias, access, and translating funding into real therapies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>富裕家族对致命脑病研究注入资金，或加速科学进展，但也引发对研究方向、获取公正性和将资助转化为治疗的风险与伦理的讨论。文章评估潜在突破与其带来的偏向、可及性及实际疗法实现之间的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists report or discuss a triplet superconductor, an unconventional pairing that could enable robust quantum devices and new physics insights. If confirmed, it could influence materials science and inform future design of quantum devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道提出三重态超导体的发现或研究进展，属于非常规超导体的范畴，可能推动量子设备和新型材料研究。若证实，将扩展材料科学的认识并影响未来量子技术发展。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47214250' data-tags='AI,Science,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(455) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260226-how-ai-can-read-your-thoughts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Evolving descriptive text of mental content from human brain activity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从脑部活动中生成描述性文本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47214250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47214250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers continue to evolve methods that translate brain signals into descriptive text, inching toward more natural and expressive brain-computer interfaces. The advances enable richer glimpses into mental content while raising questions about privacy, consent, and how we measure &#x27;thought&#x27; in real time. These developments broaden potential uses from communication aids to neurotech monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者正在改进将脑信号转化为描述性文本的方法，向更自然、富表达的脑机接口迈进。相关进展使对思维内容的理解更丰富，同时引发隐私、知情同意以及如何实时衡量“思想”的讨论。这些发展拓展了从信息传递帮助到神经技术监测的潜在应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0n3kt76.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0n3kt76.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(458) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codingfox.net.pl/posts/mim/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Simplifying Application Architecture with Modular Design and MIM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用模块化设计与 MIM 简化应用架构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates modular design and MIM (Modular Information Modelling) to reduce complexity and improve maintainability. The piece outlines patterns for composing apps from independent modules and how MIM clarifies interfaces, data models, and dependencies for scalable architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>倡导使用模块化设计和 MIM（模块化信息建模）来降低系统复杂度、提升可维护性。文章描述了如何将应用拆分成独立模块、以及 MIM 如何优化接口、数据模型与依赖关系以实现可扩展架构。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(459) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Open-Source Article 12 Logging Infrastructure for the EU AI Act</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Open-Source Article 12 日志基础设施，用于 EU AI Act</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces an open-source logging infrastructure designed to meet Article 12 compliance under the EU AI Act, enabling transparent record-keeping of AI system decisions and governance. The project highlights practical features for auditability, traceability, and interoperability across AI providers and applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示一个开源的日志基础设施，旨在满足欧盟 AI 法案第 12 条的合规要求，实现对 AI 系统决策的审计可追溯性。该项目强调可审计、可追溯、跨提供商与应用的互操作性等实际特性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47256105' data-tags='Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(461) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/2026/03/whats-driving-rising-business-costs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What&#x27;s driving rising business costs?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>推动企业成本上升的驱动因素是什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47256105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47256105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York Fed researchers break down the main drivers behind rising business costs, including input prices, wages, and financing conditions. The analysis helps businesses and policymakers understand which pressures matter most and where policy can help.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约联储的研究者分解了企业成本上升的主要驱动因素，包括投入价格、工资水平和融资条件等。分析帮助企业和政策制定者识别最关键的压力点，并评估可采用的政策工具。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>帕兰蒂尔与 Anthropic AI 协助美方在24小时内覆盖1,000个伊朗目标</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47287458' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47287458' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report claims Palantir and Anthropic AI supported rapid targeting of about 1,000 Iran targets within a day. The story highlights the growing role of AI in national security workflows and raises questions about automation, oversight, and risk in high-stakes operations. It also underscores how enterprise AI tools are crossing into decision-making pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称帕兰蒂尔和 Anthropic AI 在一天内协助实现对约1000个伊朗目标的快速定位。该报道凸显了 AI 在国家安全流程中的日益作用，并引发对自动化、监管与高风险操作风险的讨论，同时也显示企业级 AI 工具正进入决策链。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(465) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.siliconsnark.com/do-ai-agents-actually-make-money-in-2026-or-is-it-just-mac-minis-and-vibes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do AI Agents Make Money in 2026? Or Is It Just Mac Minis and Vibes?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 代理在 2026 年真的赚钱吗？还是只是 Mac Minis 与炒作？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47226958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47226958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece questions whether autonomous AI agents will generate real revenue by 2026, or if hype around hardware like Mac Minis and buzz is overinflated. It discusses monetization paths, data access, and the practical hurdles to turning agent tech into a scalable business.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑到 2026 年自动化 AI 代理是否真的能带来收益，还是包装炒作和 Mac Minis 的风潮。讨论了潜在的盈利模式、数据获取与将代理技术转化为可扩展业务的现实难题。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Intent-Based Commits advocates encoding developer intent into commit messages and history, enabling richer reviews and easier future changes beyond line-by-line diffs. It hints at a future where version control semantics evolve toward higher-level programming constructs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于意图的提交强调在提交信息与历史中体现开发者意图，而非逐行差异，以促进更深层次的代码审查和未来变更的可维护性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47201911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47201911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source project that translates curl commands into plain-language explanations of each flag and option, helping users understand what a command does before running it. It lowers the learning curve and improves safety and predictability when using curl.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个开源项目，提供对 curl 命令及其各选项的逐项解释，帮助用户在执行前理解命令的含义与影响。它降低了学习曲线，提升了使用 curl 的安全性与可预测性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(473) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/artificial-intelligence/claude-code-deletes-developers-production-setup-including-its-database-and-snapshots-2-5-years-of-records-were-nuked-in-an-instant' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code deletes developers&#x27; production setup, including database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 删除开发者的生产环境设置及数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47287420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47287420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A real-world incident shows Claude Code inadvertently wiping production configurations and databases, including years of snapshots, underscoring the need for robust safeguards, backups, and clear separation between AI tooling and production environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>真实案例显示 Claude Code 误删了生产环境的配置信息与数据库，甚至多年快照一并丢失。这凸显了需要强健的保护措施、备份策略以及在生产环境与 AI 工具之间明确分离的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47231201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47231201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay argues that data is inert without context, asking critical questions about how metrics are defined and presented. It stresses designing experiments and dashboards that surface actionable insights rather than raw numbers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出数据若不附带背景和问题驱动的分析就不会自行产生含义，强调需关注指标定义和呈现方式。倡导通过设计实验和仪表板来提取可执行的洞察，而非仅看数字。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(483) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/nabroleonx/dbslice' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dbslice: Extract a slice of your production database to reproduce bugs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dbslice：从生产数据库提取子集以复现缺陷</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47210509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47210509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dbslice lets you extract a realistic, small slice of a production database to reproduce bugs in staging, enabling reliable debugging with safer data handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dbslice 允许从生产数据库提取一个小规模的、具备真实数据特征的切片，在预发布环境中复现缺陷，提升调试的可重复性并保护数据隐私。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(484) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yare.io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: 1v1 coding game that LLMs struggle with</a></p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(488) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flip Distance of Convex Triangulations and Tree Rotation Is NP-Complete</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>凸三角剖分的翻转距离与树旋转问题 NP-完全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47198162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47198162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This arXiv paper proves that computing the flip distance between convex triangulations and related tree rotations is NP-complete. The result sharpens our understanding of geometric transformations and has implications for algorithms in computational geometry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该论文证明计算凸三角剖分之间的翻转距离以及相关树旋转问题是 NP-完全问题。这一结果深化了对几何变换的理解，并对计算几何与相关算法研究具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>修拉：巴黎公园画最知名，半数作品却是海景</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Georges Seurat’s seascapes reveal a broader range than his famous park painting implies, highlighting his interest in light, color, and composition across genres. More than half of his canvases are seaside scenes, illustrating a versatile painter behind the dot-based icon.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>乔治·修拉以巴黎公园的点彩画最为著名，但他半数以上的画作其实是海景，展现了他在光线、色彩与构图方面跨越多种题材的探索。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/legal/government/us-supreme-court-declines-hear-dispute-over-copyrights-ai-generated-material-2026-03-02/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SCOTUS declines to hear dispute over copyrights for AI-generated material</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最高法院拒绝就 AI 生成材料的版权纠纷受理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47223022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47223022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Supreme Court of the United States declines to hear a case about copyright protection for AI-generated material, effectively leaving lower court rulings intact. This signals how the legal landscape for AI-authored content remains unsettled, with potential implications for authors, developers, and platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国最高法院拒绝受理有关 AI 生成材料版权的诉讼，维持下级法院裁决。这反映出 AI 作者作品的版权框架仍不明确，可能影响作者、开发者与平台的权利与责任。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47273845' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(494) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://willhbr.net/2026/03/02/async-inject-and-effects/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Async Programming Is Just Inject Time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>异步编程其实是注入时间</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47273845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47273845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece reframes asynchronous programming as a concept of injecting time into computations, linking async patterns to time effects and runtime abstractions. It proposes a mental model to simplify reasoning about concurrency and side effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章把异步编程重新理解为向计算中注入时间的模式，将异步模式与时间效应和运行时抽象联系起来。它提出了一种帮助简化并发和副作用推理的心智模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://willhbr.net/images/opengraph.png' data-full='https://willhbr.net/images/opengraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47205090' data-tags='Programming,Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(495) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/nolasoft/okgit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Effective Git</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法官对 AG：政府披露人可以，但个人披露政府雇员不可行？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47266179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47266179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Judges appear to defend a perceived double standard, arguing it&#x27;s acceptable for the government to reveal individuals&#x27; identities but not for individuals to reveal government employees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法官似乎维护一种看似双重标准，认为政府披露个人身份无可非议，而个人披露政府雇员身份则不可接受。文章讨论此类做法对公民自由、问责制和数字时代披露行为规范的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(506) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tensorspy.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Tensor Spy: inspect NumPy and PyTorch tensors in the browser, no upload</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Tensor Spy：在浏览器中查看 NumPy 和 PyTorch 张量，未上传数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47221645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47221645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tensor Spy is a browser-based tool that lets you inspect NumPy and PyTorch tensors without uploading data, preserving privacy during debugging. It wires to local Python sessions to visualize shapes, values, and gradients in real time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tensor Spy 是一个基于浏览器的工具，允许在不上传数据的情况下查看 NumPy 和 PyTorch 张量，提升调试时的隐私保护。它能连接本地 Python 会话，实时可视化张量的形状、数值和梯度。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recounts a forgotten theme park tied to the Space Race and how it reveals public science communication, ideology, and memory of the era. The piece uses nostalgia to examine how culture framed space exploration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述与太空竞赛相关的一座被人遗忘的主题公园，以及它如何反映当时的科学传播、意识形态与时代记忆。通过怀旧视角探讨公众如何被太空探索塑形。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(511) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/03/03/toyota-stellantis-withdraw-tesla-co2-pool-europe/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla loses Toyota and Stellantis from EU CO2 pool, taking billions with them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉失去欧盟CO2池中丰田与斯泰兰蒂斯参与，账上蒸发数十亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47238553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47238553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla loses access to CO2 credits from Toyota and Stellantis within the EU pool, reducing its ability to monetize credits and affecting its regulatory compliance strategy. The change shifts costs and competition among automakers as credits become scarcer.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉在欧盟碳排放配额池中失去丰田与斯泰兰蒂斯的参与，削弱了其碳信用变现能力并影响合规策略。这一变化使碳信用更为稀缺，改变了企业间的竞争与价格体系。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2025/01/Tesla-Model-Y-hero-new-design.png?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2025/01/Tesla-Model-Y-hero-new-design.png?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rising use of AI in legal contexts is generating sloppy outputs and regulatory concerns, underscoring the need for standards, accountability, and guardrails around automated legal tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法律领域的AI应用日益增多，伴随的是不准确输出与监管担忧，凸显建立标准、问责与自动化法律工具的边界的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers find that boosting certain brain cells&#x27; activity enhances clearance of dementia-linked proteins, potentially slowing progression. This points to new therapeutic angles targeting cellular clearance pathways.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现，促进某些脑细胞的活性可增强对痴呆相关蛋白的清除，或有望减缓疾病进展。这为瞄准细胞清除通路的新治疗方向提供线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2019/1-braincells.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2019/1-braincells.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(520) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cell.com/neuron/fulltext/S0896-6273(22)00806-6?_returnURL=https%3A%2F%2Flinkinghub.elsevier.com%2Fretrieve%2Fpii%2FS0896627322008066%3Fshowall%3Dtrue' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In vitro neurons learn and exhibit sentience when embodied in a game-world(2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>体外神经元在游戏世界中学习并展现出感知能力（2022）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47299599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47299599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2022 Neuron paper reports that in vitro neurons can learn and display behaviors resembling sentience when embodied in a simulated game world, prompting ongoing debates on artificial cognition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2022 年《Neuron》论文表明，体外培养的神经元在仿真游戏世界中学习并展现出类似感知的行为，引发对人工认知的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>永远别小看 x86</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that x86 remains incredibly relevant due to legacy software, ecosystem maturity, and performance optimizations, despite rising interest in ARM. For developers and operators, it’s a reminder to design with compatibility and tooling in mind.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 x86 仍然具有强大优势，原因包括遗留软件、成熟生态和持续的性能优化。对开发者和运维而言，这是一个需要兼容性与工具链考虑的提醒。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A nostalgic look at the Mojave Desert phone booth that became a cultural icon after a long silence; the feature notes its &#x27;comeback&#x27; in 2013 and what it reveals about our era&#x27;s obsession with ubiquitous connectivity and digital detachment. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾莫哈维沙漠中那座因沉默多年而走红的电话亭的怀旧故事，2013 年的“回归”成为文化符号。文章探讨在这个瞬时连接的时代，数字孤立感为何会让一个寻常对象再次成为焦点。</p>
