<p class='hn-title'>(532) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codon.org.uk/~mjg59/blog/p/to-update-blobs-or-not-to-update-blobs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>To update blobs or not to update blobs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>要不要更新二进制 Blob？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47231745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47231745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A philosophical look at whether and when to update binary blobs in a content-addressed storage system. It discusses implications for reproducibility, caching, and provenance when data evolves.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在内容可寻址存储体系中，权衡何时更新二进制 blob 的问题。讨论更新带来的可复现性、缓存与溯源等影响。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47304146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 6</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47304146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla has opened its first Megacharger station dedicated to Semi truck customers in California, expanding high-speed charging for heavy-duty electric freight. The move signals scaling of dedicated charging networks for long-haul EVs and could influence fleet logistics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉在加州为 Semi 客户开放了首个 Megacharger 充电站，扩展了重型电动货车的高功率充电网络。这标志着面向长途运输的专用充电网络正在规模化，可能影响车队的运输计划与运营。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(535) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://leancrew.com/all-this/2025/12/some-lotto-math/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Some Lotto Math</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一些乐透数学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47248801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 6</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47248801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This post walks through lottery odds and expected value, showing why lottery tickets are a poor long-run bet even when jackpots grow large. Probability and math explain why you shouldn&#x27;t expect profits from buying tickets. The takeaway is a sober reminder about risk vs reward in lotteries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章解析乐透彩票的赔率与期望值，说明即便头奖再大，长线下注彩票仍通常不是盈利的好方法。通过概率和数学原理，揭示买票并不能带来稳定回报。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47299625' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(540) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://systemic.engineering/ai-needs-identity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;I Can&#x27;t Do That, Dave&quot; – No Agent Yet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《I Can&#x27;t Do That, Dave》：尚无代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47299625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47299625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that current AI systems are not true agents with persistent identity and goals. It examines why agent-like autonomy raises safety and control concerns and what &#x27;identity&#x27; would mean for AI agents. The author concludes that building usable, safe autonomous agents remains an unsolved challenge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为当前的AI系统并非具备持续身份和目标的真正代理。它探讨了具备代理式自主性的安全与可控性问题，以及‘身份’在AI代理中的含义。作者认为构建可用且安全的自主代理仍然是一个尚未解决的难题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://systemic.engineering/content/images/2026/01/logo_white_circle-1.png' data-full='https://systemic.engineering/content/images/2026/01/logo_white_circle-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
